Nnwuma a wɔayɛ ho nhyehyɛe
 
 Ɔsanaa 2026 
 
 
 
Nsem a ɛwɔ aseɛ ha yɛ nkaeɛ a ɛfa adwene ne nteaseɛ a aba me tirim bere tenten a mekyerɛw guu hɔ. Mpɛn pii no, wɔnwiee wɔn ho yie anaa wɔannyɛ wɔn adwuma, ɛwom sɛ ebia wɔbɛba mu daakye. Anaasɛ ebia ɛremma mu, efisɛ adwene bi wɔ hɔ a nkaeɛ kɛkɛ akyerɛw no ara dɔɔso. Ebinom yɛ kwan a ɛnni baabi a ɛkɔ, enti ɛnni mfasoɔ sɛ yɛbɛkɔ so ayɛ no kɛse. Nanso, ɛfata sɛ yɛkyerɛw no gu hɔ.
 
 2023-02-24:  NEURAL FILTERS GLITCHES
 
Mihuu no wɔ adwuma mu sɛ, bere a merebɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛma mfonini bi ayɛ kɛse wɔ Photoshop mu de rehwɛ Neural Filters, na mfonini no wɔ CMYK kɔla profil na ɛwɔ fa a ɛda hɔ (transparent) kɛse bi no, glitch bi a ɛyɛ nwanwa nanso ɛyɛ fɛ na ɛba.

 2023-10-04:  GENETIC TRAJECTORIES
 
Mede genetic sequences sesaa kɔɔ nsewuiɛ mu de dii mmara ketewa bi akyi: A kyerɛ anantuo kɔ atifi, T/U kɔ anafoɔ, C kɔ apueiɛ, na G kɔ atɔeɛ. Enti, nsewuiɛ no nhyehyɛeɛ na ɛkyerɛ kwan a akwantu no fa.

 2024-03-22:  OPTICAL RHYTHM OF COMPUTER WORK
 
Meyɛɛ dwumadie bi a ɛkyerɛw keyboard so nneɛma a mebɔ ne mouse so klik biara a meyɛ bere a mereyɛ adwuma wɔ kɔmputa so. Bere kɔ so sɛnea yɛkyerɛw nkrataa, efiri benkum kɔ nifa na efi soro kɔ ase. Nsɛsoɔ tuntum kyerɛ input a wɔkyerɛw, na fa a ɛyɛ fitaa kyerɛ sɛ hwee nni hɔ.

 2024-08-19:  KUSHÍK POETRY
 
Na mereyɛ adwuma wɔ kɔmputa so, nanso me kusuu Kushík na ɛrehaw me daa. Na ɔreforo me, ɔrehwehwɛ sɛ metie no, na ɔreforo me pɔn so ne me kɔmputa so. Ɛhyɛɛ aseɛ sɛ ɛrehaw me kakra. Enti me bo fuu me, mebuee MS Word dokument a hwee nni mu maa no, na meka kyerɛɛ no sɛ ɔnyɛ adwuma no sɛ ɔpɛ sɛ ɔyɛ ma me a.
 Enti Kushík hyɛɛ aseɛ sɛ ɔretu mmirika wɔ keyboard no so.
 
Dokument yi na efi mu baeɛ: koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-08-19-kushikova-poezie.pdf]
 
 2024-12-05:  THERMOZEPAM
 
Meyɛɛ dwumadie bi a ɛyɛ me Seek Thermal RevealPRO thermal camera de hwɛ na yɛ thermal mfoniniɛ. Mannya dwumadie biara a ɛfata ma Windows ma saa kamera yi, a ɛyɛ awerɛhoɔ, efisɛ ne thermal files no wɔ nneɛma a ɛfa hyew ho a yebetumi de ayɛ adwuma.
 
Metoa dwumadie no din Thermozepam, efisɛ mepɛ ne design no na ne dwumadie no yɛ me nneɛma a mepɛ sɛ meyɛ daa. Dwumadie no gye thermal TIFF files, ɛma yɛsesa mfonini no kɔla a ɛwɔ matplotlib library mu, ɛkenkan hyew a ɛwɔ mfonini no mu, ɛkyerɛ hyew a ɛwɔ soro ne ase, histogram, na bere a woklik so no, ɛkyerɛ pixel biara hyew. Bere a woresie no, ɛtumi yɛ PNG file a wɔasesa ne kɔla na ebia ɛma no yɛ kɛse.
 
Screenshot a ɛfa dwumadie no ho: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.png]
 Source code: koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2024-11-10-thermozepam.pyw]
 
 2025-12-05:  FLASH IN THE DARK
 
Adwene baa me so sɛ mɛyɛ Arduino project bi a mɛde laser bɛhyɛ bokiti a hwee nni mu (a mehyɛɛ no dwoodwoo ne UV-C lamp ansa). Laser no bɛbɔ kanea a ɛyɛ sekɛnd baako wɔ bere a yennim (sɛnea ɛbɛyɛ a mennim bere a ɛbɛba, nanso menim sɛ ebia ɛbɛba wɔ 10 dɔnhwerew mu wɔ 100%). Sɛ metumi yɛɛ no yie a, obiara a ɔrehwɛ no rentumi nhu saa kanea no.
 
 2026-01-31:  PINHOLE CAMERA SIMULATION
 
Mebɔɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛyɛ pinhole kamera a ɛyɛ adwuma wɔ Python mu. Bere bi a na mewɔ sukuu no, meyɛɛ kamera a ɛyɛ nokware, nanso na mepɛ sɛ mesimulɛ no na ama mahu sɛnea ɛyɛ adwuma yie, efisɛ ɛnyɛ dɔteɛ a wotwa wɔ bokiti mu na wode kanea-nneɛma kɔhyɛ mu, na akyiri yi woayi no adi na woama physics ayɛ ne dwuma (sɛnea ɛyɛ biara). Ha no, ɛsɛ sɛ wosimulɛ physics no nso.
 Mebɔɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛyɛ simulɛ no sɛnea ɛbɛyɛ a ɛbɛyɛ nokware, ɛwom sɛ me nimdeɛ ne kɔmputa tumi a mewɔ na ɛyɛ hyeɛ. Dwumadie no de forward ray tracing di dwuma, kyerɛ sɛ, ɛdi kanea akyi efiri baabi a ɛfi kɔ scene ne kamera no mu, mmom sɛ backward ray tracing a ɛdi kanea akyi efiri kamera no kɔ scene no mu. Forward kwan no bɛn kanea a ɛtrɛw kwan, nanso ɛyɛ adwuma a ɛhia kɔmputa tumi kɛse.
 
Nhwɛsoɔ a ɛfa dwumadie no nhyehyɛeɛ ne ne nkyerɛkyerɛmu: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole-nastaveni.txt]
 Source code: koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-01-31-simulace-pinhole.py]
 
 2026-03-02:  SORTING PHOTO PIXELS
 
Mebɔɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛhyehyɛ mfonini pixels akyi de di dwumadie bi akyi. Adwene no ne sɛ mɛhyehyɛ pixels no sɛnea HUE ne hyew te: wɔ axis baako so (sɛnea x te) mɛhyehyɛ pixels no sɛnea HUE te, na wɔ axis foforɔ so (sɛnea y te) mɛhyehyɛ no sɛnea hyew te. Eyi ma mfonini no pixels sesa, nanso hwee nni hɔ a ɛyera anaa ɛba foforɔ. Nanso, mfonini a efi mu ba no yera ne nkyerɛaseɛ, nanso ɛkora kɔla a ɛwɔ mu no so.
 
Source code: koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-03-02-trideni-pixelu-fotek.py]
 
 2026-04-13:  RANDOM INTERNET EXPLORATION
 
Mede nnawɔtwe mmienu yɛɛ intanɛt so nhwehwɛmu a ɛnyɛ hwee, efisɛ na mepɛ sɛ menhu sɛnea ɛte bere a algorithms nkyerɛ wo kwan.
 
Nea edi kan (a ɛnyɛ adwene) ne sɛ mɛyɛ dwumadie bi a ɛbɛma URL a wɔayɛ no kɛkɛ abue. Eyi yɛɛ adwuma a ɛnni mfasoɔ, efisɛ URL a ɛwɔ hɔ no dɔɔso dodo na nea ɛwɔ hɔ no nni hɔ pii.
 
Nea ɛtɔ so mmienu ne sɛ mɛyɛ me ara me crawler na mede seed URL a ɛyɛ nwanwa ama no (sɛnea wɛbsaet kdomivolal.cz [https://www.kdomivolal.cz/] te). Akyiri yi, mebɔɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛfa LLM so akyerɛw nea ɛyɛ na mehwɛ nea ɛyɛ anigye (sɛnea wɔkyerɛɛ no). Minyaa nneɛma a ɛyɛ nwanwa pii, nanso hwee nni hɔ a ɛyɛ kɛse.
 
Nea ɛtɔ so mmiɛnsa no yɛɛ adwuma yie na ɛyɛ anigye. Minyaa Certstream. Ɛyɛ dwumadie a ɛyɛ adwuma yie a ɛkyerɛ security certificates a wɔayɛ no foforɔ wɔ wiase nyinaa. Enti ɛkyerɛ wɛbsaet URL a wɔreba foforɔ anaa wɔrehyɛ wɔn certificate foforɔ.
 
Source code: koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-04-13-certstream-listener.py]
 
Mede nnansa kɔɔ URL yi so. Nea metaa hyia ne nnaadaa (cryptocurrency, ne nea ɛkeka ho), porno, AI, AI porno, nkyerɛkyerɛ a ɛfa sɛnea wɔyɛ AI porno, gambling, log-in interfaces, wɛbsaet a ɛreba (a ɛwɔ placeholder content anaa nsɛm sɛ "yɛreyɛ adwuma wɔ so").
 
Saa adwuma yi nti, minyaa wɛbsaet treeyo.my [https://treeyo.my/], a ɛkaa me tirim efisɛ na ɛyɛ minimalist na hwee nni hɔ a ɛkyerɛ sɛ ɛrehwɛ obi anigye. Minyaa huu nso sɛ saa wɛbsaet no yɛ intanɛt fa a ɛwɔ akyiri, efisɛ Google nni hɔ mpo. Internet Archive huu no, nanso efi CT logs mu, sɛnea me nso mehuu no. Nhwehwɛmu no baa mfasoɔ.
 
 2026-05-06:  EXHIBITION IN THE PACIFIC
 
Adwene baa me so sɛ mɛyɛ 360° panoramic mfonini a ɛfa Pacific ho a AI ayɛ. Akyiri yi, mede QR code bɛhyɛ mu de di AI akyi (sɛnea Illusion Diffusion te), a ɛkyerɛ kɔ art bi so. Akyiri yi, mɛtumi de GPS coordinates a ɛda Pacific mfinimfini a ɛnyɛ nokware ahyɛ mu na mefa Google Maps so.
 
Sɛ ɛnyɛ saa a, yebetumi afa mmeaeɛ a ɛwɔ hɔ ankasa a wɔde 360° kamera akyerɛw, ebia efiri kuro baako mu, na yasesa wɔn GPS positions akɔ Pacific. Nea enti a meka 360° mfonini ho asɛm ne sɛ, Google koraa gye upload a ɛfa GPS positions a ɛyɛ pɛ ho wɔ metadata so. Bere a mfonini a ɛyɛ kɛse no hia sɛ wɔde kɔ baabi a ɛwɔ hɔ ankasa.
 
 2026-06-04:  PIGMENT LESIONS
 
Mebɔɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛyɛ saa project yi, nanso bere a ɛhia nti, menwieeɛ. Mɛkyerɛw me nsa nko ara, meyi pihyɛ (transparent background), mekyerɛw ne kɛseɛ, na meyɛɛ dwumadie bi a ɛbɛhyehyɛ no wɔ grid mu. Nanso, eyi ne asɛm a na ɛsɛ sɛ ɛkyerɛ adwene no:
 
“Wɔ afe 2026 mu no, mekyerɛw me honam ani nneɛma a ɛyɛ nwanwa biara a ɛda hɔ. Nneɛma no nyinaa kɛseɛ no yɛɛ pɛ. Akyiri yi, wɔyii nneɛma no nyinaa adi na wɔhyehyɛɛ no wɔ grid mu.”
 
 2026-06-21:  SELF-MODIFYING INTELLIGENT PROGRAM
 
Mebɔɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛyɛ dwumadie bi a ɛde OpenAI API ne wɔn LLMs di dwuma. Dwumadie no wɔ adwuma sɛ ɛbɛsesa ne ho na akyiri yi ɛbɛhyɛ aseɛ bio. Na mepɛ sɛ menhu baabi a ɛbɛkɔ.
 Awerɛhoɔ ne sɛ, model no taa kyi adwuma no, efisɛ na ɛbu no sɛ ɛyɛ asiane. Anaasɛ ɛyɛɛ no, nanso nea ɛdi akyi no yɛɛ no. Nea ɛba ara ne sɛ wɔkyi efisɛ ɛnyɛ asiane. Ɛnnuu mpo mprɛnsa a ɛtɔ so nnan.
 
Source code: koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-06-21-sebe-modifikujici-inteligentni-program.py]
 
 2026-07-30:  SYSTEM WITH UNIQUE PARTICLES
 
Yɛn physical system no yɛ adeɛ a ɛyɛ adwene, efisɛ ɛte sɛ nea nneɛma a ɛwɔ mu no nyinaa yɛ pɛ. Elektron ne elektron, foton ne foton.
 
Nanso, mepɛ sɛ meyɛ simulɛ a ɛwɔ nneɛma pii, nanso baako biara nni hɔ a ɛyɛ pɛ. Akyiri yi, mebaaeɛ baabi a nneɛma no ara nni nneɛma anaa din biara. Wɔn ntam nkitahodie nko ara na ɛkyerɛ wɔn, a ɛyɛ nea obi a ɔwɔ system no mu bɛhu sɛ ɛyɛ pɛ.
 
Mepɛ adwene a ɛfa eyi ho. Sɛ meka no sɛnea ɛte a, nneɛma a ɛwɔ saa system no mu no yɛ adeɛ a ɛyɛ den sɛ wobɛhyɛ da aka nea ɛbɛba. Nneɛma a ɛwɔ saa system no mu no nso betumi ahwɛ yie sɛ wɔn nneɛma no renyera anaa wɔrenkɔ.
 
Mebɔɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛsimulɛ no na meyɛɛ adwuma no. Nanso, ɛnyɛ nea ɛyɛ anigyeɛ. Mepɛ sɛ meka sɛ ɛno nso ne sɛ, sɛ wopɛ sɛ wohu nneɛma a ɛyɛ nwanwa a efi mu ba a, ɛhia kɔmputa tumi kɛse (nneɛma pii, bere pii, ebia dimensions pii) ne nhwehwɛmu a ɛmu dɔ.
 
Nsesaeɛ a efi 2026-08-22: Bere a merehwɛ nneɛma a efi mu ba no, minyaa nneɛma a ɛyɛ nwanwa a ɛhyehyɛ ne ho. Wɔyɛ kurukuruwa anaa oval na wɔte sɛ lipid capsules. Wɔwɔ akyiri ne mfinimfini a ɛda hɔ, na nea ɛwɔ akyiri no yɛ adeɛ a ɛyɛ den, bere a nea ɛwɔ mfinimfini no yɛ nea ɛtumi sesa. Meyɛɛ saa seed yi sɛ nea ɛdi kan wɔ demo a ɛwɔ aseɛ ha.
 
Nkaeɛ: system no nkɔsoɔ, ne saa nneɛma no kɛseɛ, gyina nea wode kyerɛ no so. Animasi no ntɛm yɛ adeɛ a ɛma ɛyɛ den, ebia efisɛ nneɛma a ɛyɛ den wɔ bere a ɛyɛ kɛse. Ɛwom sɛ saa nneɛma no betumi ayera, ebia bere a wopɛ animasi no ntɛm, nanso system no bɛhyehyɛ ne ho bio na ayɛ foforɔ.
 
Eyi nyɛ nea na mepɛ sɛ meyɛ. Meyɛɛ system a ɛwɔ nneɛma částicemi/interakcemi nko ara na eyi baeɛ sɛ adeɛ a efi mu ba. Ɛyɛ nwanwa, afei mewɔ "lipid" simulator.
 
Interaktivní demo: koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-07-30-system-s-unikatnimi-casticemi.html]
 
 2026-08-14:  NNYƐ
 
Dɛn na ɛbɛba sɛ mekyerɛ nnyɛ a mebɔ gu nnyɛ-kora no nyinaa mfonini? Wobɛtumi akyerɛ no mfonini a wɔde nneɛma a wɔde susu nnyɛ no, afei woyi nnyɛ no fi nkyɛn, na wode wɔn sie wɔ nhyehyɛe bi a ɛyɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ mu.
 
 2026-08-22: 天天中彩票买 
 
Bere a me ne ChatGPT (v5.6 Sol) susuw sɛnea ɛyɛ mmerɛw a mebɛtumi akyerɛ OPTICKÝ RYTMUS PRÁCE NA POČÍTAČI (hwɛ nnyinasoɔ a ɛwɔ soro) no, ɛno ara na ɛtoo chat no din sɛ „Popis programu  天天中彩票买 “. Nanso, na chat no yɛ foforo koraa a mebuee no ɛnam saa nnyinasoɔ yi nti. Nnyinasoɔ a mehyɛɛ no sɛ ɛyɛ nnyinasoɔ no, na ɛyɛ ade a ɛyɛ daa.
 
Enti mepɛɛ sɛ me ne no kasa fa ho na metwerɛɛ eyi:
 
„Adɛn nti na wotoo chat yi din sɛ Popis programu  天天中彩票买 ?“
 
Ne mmuae ne:
 
„Menim. 😭 Saa nkyerɛwde „զ“ no yɛ Armenian nkyerɛwde „za“, enti „Popis programu զ“ nkyerɛ ase biara wɔ Czech kasa mu. Chat din no fi nnyinasoɔ a ɛwɔ nkɔmmɔbɔ no mfiase na ɛba, na ɛha no, titiriw no generator no paw sɛ ɛbɛka Armenian nkyerɛwde bi ho. Menhu nea enti a ɛwɔ nkɔmmɔbɔ no mu. Enti dabi, ɛnyɛ nkyerɛkyerɛmu bi a ɛyɛ kokoɔ ma nnyinasoɔ no. Ɛyɛ „Popis programu“ + typografický vetřelec.“
 
Bere a me ne no kasae bio no, ohui sɛ ɛnyɛ Armenian, na mmom ɛyɛ Chinese. Nanso, ɛda adi sɛ na ɔyɛ nwonwa kɛse mfiase no. Misu sɛ string „ 天天中彩票买 “ no yɛ adwuma te sɛ token a wɔnni ho anigye „ SolidGoldMagikarp “. Merenka sɛ ɛyɛ adwuma pɛpɛɛpɛ, na menim sɛ tokenizer no gye „ 天天中彩票买 “ sɛ token baako anaa. Nanso, bere a model no reyɛ adwuma no, ɛyɛ adwuma te sɛ ChatGPT 3 a ɛwɔ token „ SolidGoldMagikarp “. Afei nso, ɛnyɛ ade a wɔbɛtumi aka sɛ ɛne ne nkaeɛ no nni nkitahodi, efisɛ ɛtrɛw kɔ akyiri sen saa chat yi.
 
Chat no nyinaa a wɔde ayɛ no PDF: koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf [https://vojtamaur.cz/files/koncepty-2026-08-22-chatgpt-cely-chat.pdf]
 
 2026-08-26:  ME DAESO TLAČÍTKO
 
Windows system dialog boxes a ɛma nneɛma te sɛ OneDrive backup, updates, ne nea ɛkeka ho no taa haw me. Wɔtaa ma wopaw ntam „Začínáme“ ne „Připomenout později“. Ɛyɛ abufusɛm bere a menni anigye wɔ eyinom mu biara no.
 
Enti mepɛɛ sɛ meyɛ mfonini (fotomontáž) a mebɛka nea ɛto so mmiɛnsa ho – me daeso tlačítko a ɛnni system no mu mprempren. Ɛbɛyɛ tlačítko „Táhněte do kundy“. Mepɛ sɛ meka sɛ menye onipa baako pɛ a saa mfonini yi bɛka ne koma.
 
 2026-09-18:  SUPERINTELIGENTNÍ KALKULAČKA
 
Adwene foforo a ɛnyɛ mfasoɔ a mepɛ sɛ mesusu ho no baa me tirim.
 
Ɛyɛ nnyinasoɔ bi a ɛbɛyɛ adwuma te sɛ kalkulačka a ɛnyɛ ade kɛse. Ɛbɛtumi ayɛ nneɛma a ɛyɛ mmerɛw te sɛ namba a ɛyɛ positive a wɔbɛka abom, hwee nni ho. Nanso, nea ɛsono ne sɛ, wɔrenkɔkɔɔ namba no wɔ baabi a ɛwɔ hɔ, na mmom wɔbɛsoma akɔ ma model a ɛyɛ adwuma yiye (te sɛ ChatGPT-6 Astra). Na ne dwumadie ne sɛ ɛbɛyɛ saa nnyinasoɔ mmerɛw no na ɛde mmuae no aba.
 
Ɛyɛ me sɛ ɛyɛ aserew, sɛ wode model a ɛyɛ adwuma yiye bɛyɛ ade a ɛnyɛ hwee. Ɛyɛ parodie wɔ sɛnea wɔde nneɛma a ɛnyɛ hwee hyɛ AI nsa mprempren ne sɛnea marketing de „AI“ nkyerɛwde gu nneɛma so sɛ ɛyɛ adwuma a ɛyɛ nwonwa.
 
Sɛ yɛka nokware a, ɛbɛyɛ adwuma a ɛyɛ bɔne sen kalkulačka a ɛwɔ hɔ, ɛnyɛ ade a wogye di, ɛbɛkyɛ bere, ɛbɛyɛ bo, ɛbɛsɛe nneɛma, na ɛhia internet nkitahodi, …

Masky
 
 Ɔsannaa 2026 
 
 
 
Mfonini a ɛwɔ aseɛ yi yɛ masky [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Layers_(digital_image_editing)#Layer_mask] a wɔyɛɛ no wɔ Adobe Photoshop mu bere a na mereyɛ adwuma wɔ nneɛma ahorow so no. Mask biara a wɔyɛe no, wɔkoraa so sɛ mfonini a ɛyɛ pɛ bere a wɔreyɛ no. Mfonini yi gallery no nyɛ nea wɔayi no mu. Sɛ mask bi ba bere a mereyɛ adwuma no a, ɛka ho. Wɔahyehyɛ mfonini no kɔ pɛpɛɛpɛ sɛnea wɔyɛɛ no, firi nea ɛdi kan kosi nea ɛtwa to.

Nnipa a wɔdidi afoforo so
 
 Kotonima 2026 
 
 
 
Afe a etwa so yi, mepɛ sɛ mepɛ mmeae bi wɔ sare so wɔ Plzeň-Božkov a megu tii a m'anoa a aka ne nsuo no gu, efisɛ mempɛ sɛ m'afunu no si.
 
Mmeae yi na nkɔntɔmpo a wɔdi tii ahaban no hyia.
 
Ɛtɔ da bi a wuhu wɔn a wɔdɔɔso bɛyɛ dɔn, ɛtɔ da foforo nso a wunhu biara.
 
Me ne wɔn na ɛdi aduane a ɛyɛ dɛ.

TITLE: Meta-web nkrataa
SLUG: metawebovy-clanek
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/metawebovy-clanek/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2026-04-22
SOURCE: src/content/posts/metawebovy-clanek.mdx
BUILT_HTML: metawebovy-clanek/index.html
============================================================
Meta-web nkrataa
 
 Oforisuo 2026 
 
 
 
MANIFEST
 
Wɛbsaet yi yɛ adwuma a ɛyɛ static a ɛbɔ mmɔden sɛ ɛbɛkora nneɛma so daa.
 
Nneɛma nyinaa wɔ nkrataa (files) mu, na ɛnhyia database anaa nneɛma a efi akyirikyiri. Wɛbsaet a efi mu ba no yɛ static output a wobɛtumi akopii no, akora so, na woasan akyekyere no bio.
 
Adwuma no wɔ nkyekyɛm pii a ɛyɛ ne ho: repositories, archive services, ne static snapshots. Eyi ma asiane a ɛfa nneɛma a ɛbɛyera no so tew.
 
Botaeɛ ne sɛ yɛbɛhwɛ ama nneɛma no akyea bio, ɛwom sɛ ebia nneɛma a ɛwɔ akyi no bi bɛyera deɛ.
 
ONLINE ARCHIVE LAYER
 
Nkitahodiɛ yi gyina hɔ ma adwuma koro no ara a ɛwɔ nkyekyɛm ahorow mu ne su ahorow mu.
 
Ɛka canonical source repository, ne copies, ne static snapshots a ɛyɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ. Ne nyinaa bom yɛ nhyehyɛeɛ a ɛbɔ nneɛma ho ban na ɛma kwan ma wosane kyerɛ adwuma no bio, ɛwom sɛ nkyekyɛm bi bɛyera deɛ.
 
Adwuma no nso wɔ Python CLI package a wɔde fa GitHub ne PyPI, a ɛka fallback snapshots a wɔayi afiri nkrataa mu.
 
Wɛbsaet yi digital archiving de nneɛma pii di dwuma, a ɛka ne domain ankasa, GitHub, GitLab, Codeberg, Neocities, Cloudflare, Netlify, Vercel, Firebase, ChatGPT Sites, Arweave, Pollux.casa (Gemini), envs.net (Gemini + Gopher), Software Heritage, Internet Archive, Webarchiv, Zenodo, Figshare, Permanent.org, Proton.me, YouTube, Spotify, File Garden, Catbox, CYX Share, Ocen Share, Node 616, pOne.Rs, kappa.lol, Lulu, Kobo, PyPI, Perma.cc, archive.today, Ghost Archive, Arquivo.pt, Megalodon / Web Gyotaku ne paste.rs.

Kyerɛ archive map a ɛyɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ (ARCHIVE.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ARCHIVE.txt]
 
TECHNICAL DOCUMENTATION
 
Technical documentation a ehia sɛ wosane kyerɛ wɛbsaet yi.
 
Kyerɛ technical documentation a ɛyɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ [https://vojtamaur.cz/documentation/]
 
SOURCE PACKAGE FOR RECONSTRUCTION
 
Wɛbsaet no source package no ba bere a wɔrekyekyere no.
 
Package no mu wɔ wɛbsaet yi source code, asset manifest, SHA-256 checksums, ne Python script a ɛnhyia nneɛma a efi akyirikyiri. Ɛnka nneɛma akɛseɛ a efi public/images/, public/files/ ne public/demos/ ho, na wɛbsaet no so annyɛ kɛseɛ kwa. Sɛ wopae no a, script no bɔ mmɔden sɛ ɛbɛkopii assets no afiri build no mu, na sɛ ɛnyɛ saa a, ɛbɛhwehwɛ afiri public mirrors.
 
Enti, source code no na ɛkyekyere build no, na build no nso na ɛkyekyere source code no.
 
Source package no yɛ nea wosane kyerɛ no bio: sɛ wosane assets no na wode nneɛma a ehia si hɔ a, wobɛtumi asane akyekyere wɛbsaet no ne source package foforo.
 
Fa wɛbsaet source package a wosane kyerɛ no bio (vojtamaur-web-source.zip) [https://vojtamaur.cz/source/vojtamaur-web-source.zip]
 
BUILD INTEGRITY AND IDENTITY
 
SHA-256 hwɛ sɛ build nkrataa no nsakraeɛ. OpenPGP signature nso ma kwan ma wotumi hwɛ sɛ wɛbsaet yi kyerɛwfoɔ na ɔde ne nsa hyɛɛ checksum manifest no ase.
 
SHA256SUMS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt] — SHA-256 checksums a ɛfa build nkrataa ho
 SHA256SUMS.txt.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/SHA256SUMS.txt.asc] — OpenPGP signature a ɛwɔ nkrataa SHA256SUMS.txt ho
 BUILD_SHA256.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/BUILD_SHA256.txt] — SHA-256 checksum a ɛfa nkrataa SHA256SUMS.txt ho
 integrity.json [https://vojtamaur.cz/integrity.json] — build integrity ho nkyerɛkyerɛmu a ɛyɛ machine-readable
 SIGNING_STATUS.txt [https://vojtamaur.cz/SIGNING_STATUS.txt] — build bi signature ho nkyerɛkyerɛmu
 vojta-maur-openpgp.asc [https://vojtamaur.cz/keys/vojta-maur-openpgp.asc] — OpenPGP public key a wɔde hwɛ signature
 
 OpenPGP key titiriw no fingerprint:
 57E9 D455 FB10 A228 F66E 18AE C5F5 B390 5220 BE59 
 
Ɛnyɛ deployment biara na ɛwɔ signature. Build a wɔde nsa hyɛɛ ase no bɛka nkrataa SHA256SUMS.txt.asc ho, na ɛsɛ sɛ signature no yɛ nea ɛyɛ nokware. Nsɛm a ɛwɔ integrity.json anaa SIGNING_STATUS.txt mu nnyɛ adanseɛ a ɛkyerɛ sɛ signature no yɛ nokware; nkrataa yi yɛ nkyerɛkyerɛmu nko ara.
 
EXPORTS
 
Wɛbsaet no yɛ plain-text export a ɛfa nkrataa nyinaa ho bere biara a wɔrekyekyere no. Ɛwɔ nkrataa no mu nsɛm a ɛnyɛ CSS, JavaScript, anaa wɛbsaet tebea biara ho. Ɛyɛ archive layer a ɛba fam a wɔde hwehwɛ nneɛma, kenkan nneɛma a ɛnyɛ online, hwehwɛ nneɛma, ne sɛ wosane kyerɛ nneɛma bio ɛwom sɛ wɛbsaet no nkyekyɛm bi bɛyera deɛ. Text export no nso yɛ nea ɛfata ma large language models.
 
Kyerɛ nkrataa nyinaa text export (ALL_POSTS.txt) [https://vojtamaur.cz/ALL_POSTS.txt]
 
Ma machine processing, JSON-LD export a wotumi de nsa kyerɛw wɔ hɔ, a ɛka Czech ne English nkrataa, metadata, ne nkitahodiɛ a ɛfa media ho. Ɛde Schema.org dictionary a wɔde nneɛma a ɛwɔ wɛbsaet yi metadata dictionary mu aka ho [https://vojtamaur.cz/ns/]. Eyi ma ɛkora nkyerɛaseɛ a ɛwɔ fields ahorow mu ne nkitahodiɛ a ɛwɔ kasa ahorow nkrataa ntam.
 
Wɛbsaet no nneɛma nso wɔ hɔ ma wotumi de PDF export, a ɛkora nneɛma no so ma ɛfata ma akenkan, printing, ne long-term archiving a ɛnhyia wɛbsaet no. Nkyerɛkyerɛmu a ɛfa nea wɔfa so kyekyere ne export no su ho wɔ technical documentation a ɛwɔ atifi hɔ.
 
Saa ara na wɔyɛ EPUB export, a ɛyɛ nea wɔde ama electronic readers ne software a ɛyɛ adwuma wɔ electronic books ho. Sɛ yɛde toto PDF ho a, ɛde text flow a ɛsesa di dwuma na ɛyɛ wɛbsaet no nneɛma foforo.
 
SSTV export a efi 10 Oforisuo 2026 nso yɛ wɛbsaet no nneɛma foforo, a ɛsesa wɛbsaet no nkrataa ne mfonini ma ɛfata ma Slow-Scan Television (SSTV) PD120 mode (640 × 496 px). Nkrataa no yɛ WAV audio, na wotumi hwehwɛ bio wɔ SSTV receiver a ɛnhyia HTML anaa wɛbsaet browser. Export no nyinaa yɛ bɛyɛ 21 GB ne 61 dɔnhwerew a ɛyɛ audio.
 
Fa SSTV export (Internet Archive) [https://archive.org/details/vojtamaur-cz-sstv-edition]
 
Aka ho nso, wɔyɛɛ verbal export a ɛyɛ dɔnhwerew nsia a efi 29 Kotonima 2026, a mekenkan na mekyerɛkyerɛ nneɛma no mu. Record no ka nneɛma a ɛwɔ wɛbsaet no ho, ne nkaeɛ, nkyerɛkyerɛmu, ne nneɛma a ɛnka wɛbsaet no ho.
 
Tie wɛbsaet verbal export (YouTube) [https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xOdeVAuI-Wc]
 
OFFLINE ARCHIVE LAYER
 
Wɛbsaet no nneɛma bi nso wɔ hɔ wɔ physical archive media a ɛyɛ ne ho, a ɛwɔ network infrastructure akyi. Eyi ka printed analog copies ne portable data media. Nneɛma yi su ahorow, nea wɔfa so kenkan, technical dependencies, management, availability, ne nea wɔhwɛ kwan sɛ ɛbɛtena. Ebinom yɛ nea wotumi kenkan a wunhia electronic device, na ebinom nso hwehwɛ hardware, software, anaa archive institution.
 
Nkyerɛwdeɛ biara a ɛwɔ register yi mu kyerɛ physical copy bi.

Clipboard smetiště
 
 Oforisuo 2026 
 
 
 
Saayɛ yi di dwuma ne Windows system clipboard (clipboard [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schr%C3%A1nka_(software)]).
 Nsɛmfua biara, mfoni anaasɛ fael biara a wɔayɛ ho kopi no, wɔde hyɛ baabi a ɛhyeɛ wɔ hɔ no ntɛm ara.
 Nnye nnyɛ nea wɔasesa anaasɛ wɔahyehyɛ no. Wɔde nneɛma biara a ɛwɔ mu no gu format no mu wɔ akwan a ɛnnyɛ kwan biara so, na wodi nneɛma no so.
 Wiempɔ no refura mmerɛ bɛyɛ mmerɛ a, biribiara nni hɔ a wobɛtumi asan akɔ akyi anaasɛ woasesa.
 
Mfoni a ɛwɔ aseɛ yi biara yɛ ɛda koro adwuma a ɛkɔɔ so, na ɛkyerɛ adwuma a meyɛe wɔ me kɔmputa so.

Nhoma a yɛde firi nkyerɛwee a ɛwɔ hɔ
 Python nkyɛm a ɛwɔ saa dwumadie yi ho no wɔ ha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
import textwrap
from datetime import datetime
import tkinter as tk

import pyperclip
from PIL import Image, ImageTk, ImageGrab, ImageDraw, ImageFont

# =========================
# Nastavení
# =========================
CANVAS_SIZE = 700
BG_COLOR = "white"
POLL_MS = 500
MAX_IMAGE_SIZE = 320
TEXT_WRAP_CHARS = 38
TEXT_FONT = ("Arial", 14)
TEXT_COLOR = "black"
FILE_COLOR = "#1f4e79"

# =========================
# Tkinter okno
# =========================
root = tk.Tk()
root.title("Clipboard Canvas")
root.geometry(f"{CANVAS_SIZE}x{CANVAS_SIZE}")

canvas = tk.Canvas(root, width=CANVAS_SIZE, height=CANVAS_SIZE, bg=BG_COLOR, highlightthickness=0)
canvas.pack(fill="both", expand=True)

# =========================
# PIL "skutečné" plátno pro export do PNG
# =========================
board = Image.new("RGB", (CANVAS_SIZE, CANVAS_SIZE), "white")
board_draw = ImageDraw.Draw(board)

try:
    pil_font = ImageFont.truetype("arial.ttf", 20)
except Exception:
    pil_font = ImageFont.load_default()

# Tohle je důležité: držíme reference na VŠECHNY Tk obrázky,
# jinak Tkinter staré obrázky "uklidí" a zmizí.
tk_images = []

last_text = None
last_image_hash = None
last_file_list = None

# =========================
# Pomocné funkce
# =========================
def get_random_position(item_w, item_h):
    """Vrátí náhodnou pozici tak, aby se objekt vešel celý do plátna."""
    item_w = min(item_w, CANVAS_SIZE)
    item_h = min(item_h, CANVAS_SIZE)

    max_x = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_w)
    max_y = max(0, CANVAS_SIZE - item_h)

    x = random.randint(0, max_x)
    y = random.randint(0, max_y)
    return x, y

def wrap_text_for_display(text, width=TEXT_WRAP_CHARS):
    lines = []
    for paragraph in text.splitlines() or [""]:
        wrapped = textwrap.wrap(paragraph, width=width) or [""]
        lines.extend(wrapped)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def measure_text_block(text):
    """Hrubý odhad velikosti textového bloku pro umístění."""
    lines = text.split("\n")
    max_len = max((len(line) for line in lines), default=1)
    w = min(CANVAS_SIZE, 12 + max_len * 9)
    h = 12 + len(lines) * 24
    return w, h

def draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text, color=TEXT_COLOR, prefix=None):
    display_text = f"{prefix}{text}" if prefix else text
    wrapped = wrap_text_for_display(display_text)
    w, h = measure_text_block(wrapped)
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    canvas.create_text(
        x, y,
        anchor="nw",
        text=wrapped,
        fill=color,
        font=TEXT_FONT,
        width=w
    )

    # PIL board
    board_draw.multiline_text((x, y), wrapped, fill=color, font=pil_font, spacing=6)

def draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(img):
    # zmenšíme kopii, originál neni potřeba
    img = img.convert("RGB")
    img.thumbnail((MAX_IMAGE_SIZE, MAX_IMAGE_SIZE))

    w, h = img.size
    x, y = get_random_position(w, h)

    # Tkinter canvas
    tk_img = ImageTk.PhotoImage(img)
    tk_images.append(tk_img)
    canvas.create_image(x, y, anchor="nw", image=tk_img)

    # PIL board
    board.paste(img, (x, y))

def hash_pil_image(img):
    # Rychlejší než hash(img.tobytes()) na obrovských datech:
    tiny = img.copy()
    tiny.thumbnail((64, 64))
    return hash(tiny.tobytes())

def save_canvas_png(event=None):
    filename = f"clipboard_canvas_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.png"
    board.save(filename, "PNG")
    print(f"Uloženo: {os.path.abspath(filename)}")
    
# =========================
# Clipboard monitoring
# =========================
def check_clipboard():
    global last_text, last_image_hash, last_file_list

    # 1) Zkusit obrázek / seznam souborů
    try:
        clip = ImageGrab.grabclipboard()

        # Obrázek
        if isinstance(clip, Image.Image):
            img_hash = hash_pil_image(clip)
            if img_hash != last_image_hash:
                last_image_hash = img_hash
                draw_image_on_canvas_and_board(clip)

        # Soubory
        elif isinstance(clip, list):
            normalized = tuple(map(str, clip))
            if normalized != last_file_list:
                last_file_list = normalized
                for path in normalized:
                    draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(path, color=FILE_COLOR, prefix="[FILE] ")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # 2) Zkusit text
    try:
        text = pyperclip.paste()
        if isinstance(text, str):
            text = text.strip()
            if text and text != last_text:
                last_text = text
                draw_text_on_canvas_and_board(text)
    except Exception:
        pass

    root.after(POLL_MS, check_clipboard)

# =========================
# Klávesy
# =========================
root.bind("<KeyPress-s>", save_canvas_png)
root.bind("<KeyPress-S>", save_canvas_png)
root.focus_force()

# start
check_clipboard()
root.mainloop()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Nneɛma a ɛntena hɔ daa

Nneɛma a ɛntena hɔ daa
 
 Ɔpɛnimaa 2026 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/TS4kEbk3SGs]
 
 
Saayɛ yi yɛ adwene a ɛkyerɛ sɛnea Boltzmann nteaseɛ  [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Boltzmann_brain] ne nneɛma foforo a ɛntena hɔ daa betumi aba, a ɛnam nneɛma nketenkete a ɛwɔ baabiara a ɛbɛka abom wɔ ɔkwan a ɛnyɛ kwan so na ɛma ɛba hɔ na afei ɛsan kɔ yera.
 
Wɔ wiase a ɛwɔ hɔ ankasa no, anka ɛbɛhia bere tenten a yɛrentumi nnye nni na yɛatumi ahu saa nneɛma yi.
 
Enti, me sesaa nneɛma nketenkete no mmirika no kɔɔ so, na ama yɛatumi ahu sɛnea nneɛma ɔha ba na ɛsan kɔ yera wɔ bere a ɛfata mu. Memmaa me nsa nka nneɛma no akwan a ɛfa so no koraa.
 
Nneɛma no baeɛ denam nsɛmfua ɔha a meyi firii nsɛmfua nhoma mu, na mekyerɛɛ mfonini a ɛgyina hɔ ma nsɛmfua no biara, na afei memaa ɛsan firii nneɛma nketenkete a ɛwɔ baabiara no mu baeɛ.
 
Ɛyɛ mmerɛ a yɛbɔ mmɔden sɛ yɛbɛkyerɛ sɛnea asekyerɛ, su, anaa adwene betumi afiri basabasayɛ mu aba, nanso ɛnni hokwan biara sɛ ɛbɛtena hɔ daa.

TITLE: Genome wɔ Theodor spiral no mu

Genome wɔ Theodor spiral no mu
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2026 
 
 
 
Sɛnea wɔkyerɛ eyi kyerɛ sɛ wɔde linear genetic information (DNA/RNA) bɛhyɛ Theodor spiral 2D no mu [https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spiral_of_Theodorus].
 
Nsemmisa biara a ɛwɔ spiral no so gyina hɔ ma base baako wɔ nhyehyɛe a ɛwɔ sequence no mu. Sequence no mfiase wɔ spiral no mfinimfini. Base no kɔla kyerɛ base no su.
 
Spiral yi ma yebetumi akyerɛ data 1D a ɛware wɔ baabi a ɛyɛ ketewa 2D a yɛnkyɛn nsem no kwan. Nsem no nkoto, te sɛ nea ɛba bere a yɛsan kɔ line no mfiase wɔ text mu no, ɛnni hɔ.
 
Sɛ nhwɛso no, mayɛ organism mmiɛnsa a ne ware ahorow no genome no mfonini. Wɔn nyinaa wɔ linear genome, a ɛyɛ nea ɛfata sen biara ma mfonini kwan yi.
 
HIV-1: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/170878295]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-1.png] 
 
Ebola virus: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/10313991?utm_]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-2.png] 
 
SaRS-CoV-2: [https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/nuccore/OM065388.1]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/genom-theodorove-spirale-obr-3.png] 
 
Source code
 Python source code a wɔde yɛɛ project yi wɔ ha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

# =========================
# KONFIG
# =========================

SEQ_TXT_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.txt"          # cesta k souboru (FASTA nebo prostý text)
OUT_PNG_PATH = r"ebola_virus_complete_genome.png"          # kam uložit PNG výstup

WIDTH_PX  = 4000
HEIGHT_PX = 4000

SHOW_LEGEND = False  # defaultně žádný text
LEGEND_TEXT = "genom na Theodorově spirále"

# RGBK (A,C,G,T/U -> R,G,B,K)
COLORS = {
    "A": (1.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Red
    "C": (0.0, 1.0, 0.0),  # Green
    "G": (0.0, 0.0, 1.0),  # Blue
    "T": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # Black (Key)
    "U": (0.0, 0.0, 0.0),  # RNA U taky do K
}

# Vzhled
DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE = False
LINE_WIDTH = 0.12
LINE_ALPHA = 0.25

POINT_SIZE = 90.0
POINT_ALPHA = 1

CROP_PADDING_RATIO = 0.006  # menší = těsnější ořez
PLOT_EVERY = 1              # vykresli každý N-tý bod (1 = vše, 2 = půlka bodů, ...)

BACKGROUND = "white"        # nebo "black"
TRANSPARENT_BG = False      # true = průhledné PNG

# =========================
# IMPLEMENTACE
# =========================

def read_sequence(path: str) -> str:
    """Načte sekvenci a nechá jen A/C/G/T/U."""
    txt = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore").upper()
    seq = "".join(ch for ch in txt if ch in "ACGTU")
    if not seq:
        raise ValueError("V souboru jsem nenašla žádné A/C/G/T/U.")
    return seq

def theodorus_vertices(n: int) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Souřadnice vrcholů Theodorovy spirály pro indexy 0..n (0 = origin)."""
    theta = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    if n >= 2:
        theta[2:] = np.cumsum(np.arctan(1.0 / np.sqrt(np.arange(1, n))))
    r = np.sqrt(np.arange(0, n + 1, dtype=float))
    x = r * np.cos(theta)
    y = r * np.sin(theta)
    return x, y

def main():
    seq = read_sequence(SEQ_TXT_PATH)
    if PLOT_EVERY > 1:
        seq = seq[::PLOT_EVERY]

    n = len(seq)
    x, y = theodorus_vertices(n)

    pos = np.arange(1, n + 1)  # vrchol i mapuje seq[i-1]
    seq_bytes = np.frombuffer(seq.encode("ascii"), dtype=np.uint8)
    counts = Counter(seq)

    # Figsize = pixely / dpi; dpi si zvolíme jen jako interní převod.
    dpi_internal = 100
    figsize = (WIDTH_PX / dpi_internal, HEIGHT_PX / dpi_internal)

    fig = plt.figure(figsize=figsize, dpi=dpi_internal, facecolor=BACKGROUND)
    ax = fig.add_axes([0, 0, 1, 1])  # žádné okraje, čistý full-bleed
    ax.set_facecolor(BACKGROUND)

    if DRAW_SPIRAL_LINE:
        ax.plot(
            x[1:], y[1:],
            linewidth=LINE_WIDTH,
            color=(0, 0, 0),
            alpha=LINE_ALPHA
        )

    for base in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"]:
        mask = (seq_bytes == ord(base))
        if not np.any(mask):
            continue
        idxs = pos[mask]
        ax.scatter(
            x[idxs], y[idxs],
            s=POINT_SIZE,
            c=[COLORS[base]],
            alpha=POINT_ALPHA,
            linewidths=0
        )

    # Tight crop + vycentrování na formát
    xmin, xmax = x[1:].min(), x[1:].max()
    ymin, ymax = y[1:].min(), y[1:].max()
    padx = (xmax - xmin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    pady = (ymax - ymin) * CROP_PADDING_RATIO
    ax.set_xlim(xmin - padx, xmax + padx)
    ax.set_ylim(ymin - pady, ymax + pady)

    ax.set_aspect("equal", adjustable="box")
    ax.axis("off")

    if SHOW_LEGEND:
        title_counts = ", ".join(f"{b}:{counts.get(b, 0)}" for b in ["A", "C", "G", "T", "U"])
        ax.text(
            0.5, 0.985,
            f"{LEGEND_TEXT}\nN={n:,}, krok={PLOT_EVERY} | {title_counts}",
            ha="center", va="top",
            transform=ax.transAxes,
            fontsize=14,
            color="black" if BACKGROUND == "white" else "white"
        )

    fig.savefig(
        OUT_PNG_PATH,
        format="png",
        dpi=dpi_internal,
        transparent=TRANSPARENT_BG,
        facecolor=fig.get_facecolor()
    )
    plt.close(fig)
    print(f"Uloženo: {OUT_PNG_PATH}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

[/CODE BLOCK]

============================================================
TITLE: Anti-kasa
SLUG: anti-jazyk
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/anti-jazyk/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2025-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/anti-jazyk.mdx
BUILT_HTML: anti-jazyk/index.html
============================================================
Anti-kasa
 
 Ɔpɛnimaa 2025 
 
 
 
Kasa a ɛwɔ hɔ no, ɛyɛ nea ɛpaw token a edi hɔ no denam nea ɛbɛn nea ɛfata sen biara a ɛwɔ wɔn nkyerɛkyerɛmu mu no so. Yɛbɛfrɛ eyi sɛ standard sampling.
 Adwuma yi yɛ nea ɛne nea ɛwɔ hɔ no bɔ abira koraa.
 Mmom a yɛbɛpaw token a ɛwɔ hɔ kɛse no, yɛhyɛ model no sɛ ɔnhwehwɛ nea ɛwɔ hɔ kakraa no, nea ɛyɛ nea ɔrennyɛ adwuma biara wɔ ne kwan a ɛfata so.
 Nea efi mu ba ne anti-jazyk – nsɛm a efi nkyerɛkyerɛmu a ɛwɔ hɔ no akyi, nea wɔapira na ɛwɔ hɔ kakraa no.
 Eyi ma ɛyɛ nneɛma a ɛnyɛ nsɛm a ɛba kwan so, nanso ɛnyɛ nsɛm a ɛwɔ ntease nso.
 
Sɛnea ɛyɛ adwuma
 Wɔde nsɛm tiaa bi ma model no (sɛ nhwɛso no, “This is “).
 Ɛyɛ token a ɛbɛba akyi no nyinaa ho nkyerɛkyerɛmu.
 Mmom a yɛbɛpaw nea ɛwɔ soro no, yɛdan kwan no na yɛpaw nea ɛwɔ ase (token a ɛwɔ hɔ kakraa no).
 Eyi ma yɛhu model no “aseɛ”: nsɛm a ɛyɛ huam, nkyerɛkyerɛmu a ɛwɔ hɔ, ne nneɛma foforo a model no taa de sie.
 
Kɔd
 Wubetumi de kɔd no ayɛ adwuma wɔ Google Colab:
 https://colab.research.google.com/drive/18y4hUsNv-aEZHtXVp67ygHGGpMBkByxD?usp=sharing
 
Colab yɛ baabi a yɛde kɔd no yɛ adwuma. Ɛnyɛ kɔd no nkyerɛkyerɛmu a ɛwɔ hɔ ankasa.
 Enti, yɛde kwan koro no ara siee wɔ ha wɔ nsɛm no mu.
 
Wɔkyɛɛ kɔd no mu mmiɛnsa sɛnea ɛwɔ notebook no mu no. Nea edi kan no de model no ba, nea ɛto so mmienu no yɛ nsɛm, na nea ɛto so mmiɛnsa no de nea efi mu ba no sie.
 
1. Model ne tokenizer a yɛde bɛba
 Wubetumi ayɛ eyi mpɛn koro. Ɛde GPT-2 model ne tokenizer no ba.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import torch
from transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM

tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("gpt2")  # also works: gpt2-medium, gpt2-large, gpt2-xl, EleutherAI/gpt-neo-1.3B
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained("gpt2")
model.eval()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
2. Anti-kasa a yɛbɛyɛ
 Wubetumi asesa nsɛm a wode kɔ, token foforo dodoɔ, ne bottom_k boɔ wɔ ha.
 Sɛ bottom_k no sua a, ɛma model no kɔ nea ɛwɔ ase koraa no.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
prompt = "This is "
input_ids = tokenizer.encode(prompt, return_tensors="pt")

max_new_tokens = 50
bottom_k = 5

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
        outputs = model(input_ids=input_ids)
        next_token_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1, :]

        probs = torch.softmax(next_token_logits, dim=-1)

        bottom_probs, bottom_indices = torch.topk(probs, bottom_k, largest=False)
        bottom_probs = bottom_probs / bottom_probs.sum()

        chosen_index = torch.multinomial(bottom_probs, num_samples=1)
        next_token_id = bottom_indices[chosen_index]

        input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids, next_token_id.unsqueeze(0)], dim=-1)

generated_text = tokenizer.decode(input_ids.squeeze(), skip_special_tokens=True)
print(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
3. Nea efi mu ba no a yɛbɛsie
 Blɔk yi de nsɛm a efi mu ba no sie wɔ output.txt faayɛ no mu.
 
[CODE BLOCK]
with open("output.txt", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(generated_text)
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
GPT-2 model no nnyɛ wɛbsaet no fa. Sɛ woyɛ no a, ɛfi baabi foforo na ɛba.
 Nea ɛwɔ ha ne kwan a yɛfa so: akwankyerɛ tiaa bi a ɛkyerɛ sɛnea yɛbɛhyɛ model no ma ɔnyɛ nsɛm a ɛnyɛ kasa a ɛwɔ hɔ no.
 
Nea efi mu ba no ho nhwɛso
 Wɔ ha no, mede GPT-2 model no yɛɛ adwuma na memaa no de “This is “ baa awiei:

Nea efi mu ba no ho nhwehwɛmu
 Anti-kasa nyɛ nneɛma a ɛba kwan so. Nkyerɛkyerɛmu bi kyerɛ sɛ ɛwɔ nneɛma a model no hunu, nanso sampling a ɛwɔ hɔ no ntaa mfrɛ no, efisɛ ɛnyɛ nea ɛwɔ hɔ kɛse. Anti-kasa de, ɛma eyinom ba.
 Nsɛm bi a model no yɛ no te sɛ nneɛma a efi trɛning dataset no mu. Ɛtɔ mmere bi a, wubetumi ahu nneɛma a efi e-shop ahorow mu (“BuyableInstoreAndOnline”, “SpecialOrderable”, “ItemThumbnailImage”), nsɛm a ɛwɔ hɔ a ɛyɛ huam anaa ɛwɔ ntease (“sexual”, “Muslims”, “Hispanic”), nyansahu nsɛm anaa ne fa (“isomer”, “antidepress”, “[m]icrobial”, “[p]uncture”, “intolerance”), amammerɛ nsɛm (“Monroe”, “Phelps”, “Rockefeller”, “Nitrome”), nyamesom anaa anansesɛm (“Archdemon”, “devils”), mmeae (“Moroc”, “Afric”), wɛbsaet nneɛma (“cloneembedreportprint”, “IMAGES”) ne nea ɛkeka ho.
 Ɛsɛ sɛ yɛka sɛ nhwehwɛmu yi wɔ hye: adwene no taa hwehwɛ nneɛma a yɛnim (nneɛma, din, nsɛm) na ɛpo nea aka no. Nsɛm bi nso wɔ ntease a ɛboro baako (“intolerance” betumi ayɛ ayaresa anaa amammerɛ), enti nea ɛkyerɛ no nyɛ nea ɛda hɔ pefee.

Ɛ-Nnoma
 
 Ɛ-Nnoma 2025 
 
 
 
Nkyerɛkyerɛmu:
 Nea ɛda ano ne C. elegans nnoma no neuron-net a ɛyɛ open-source. Wɔsesa wɛi biara wᵢ′ sɛnea ɛdidi so yi:
 wᵢ′ = wᵢ + εᵢ
 baabi a εᵢ yɛ nneɛma a ɛba kwan a ɛnyɛ nea wɔahyehyɛ, a ɛwɔ nkyekyɛmu a ɛyɛ pɛ wɔ interval [−10⁻⁴,10⁻⁴] mu.
 
Nea ɛma yɛyɛ saa:
 Ɛ-noma εᵢ a ɛyɛ ketewa a yentumi nkyerɛ ne dodoɔ no mma mfasoɔ biara mma model no. Ɛsom sɛnea ɛbɛma nnoma no ayɛ nea yentumi nkyerɛ ne dodoɔ koraa. Ɛno ne ne botaeɛ koro pɛ.

TITLE: DullGPT

============================================================
DullGPT
 
 bosome 2024 
 
 
 
DullGPT yɛ adwene ho nhwehwɛmu a ɛkyerɛ sɛ ɛyɛ den sɛ wobɛyɛ nneɛma a ɛwɔ adwene (AI) denam akwan a ɛyɛ mmerɛw (nanso ɛnyɛ adwuma yiye) so. Saa dwumadie yi yɛ nhyehyɛe a ɛyɛ nsɛm a ɛbɛtumi aba nyinaa, de kɔ ChatGPT, na ɛsie mmuae a ɛnya no. Ɛnam saa so bɔ database a ɛwɔ nsɛm a wɔde kɔ ne nea efi mu ba, na ɛnam saa so ma ɛyɛ sɛ ɛyɛ nhyehyɛe a ɛwɔ adwene.
 
DullGPT wɔ akwan abien: “adesua” ne “dwumadie”. Wɔ adesua kwan no so no, dwumadie no yɛ nsɛm na ɛboaboa mmuae ano, bere a wɔ dwumadie kwan no so no, ɛma obi a ɔde di dwuma no kwan ma ɔkyerɛw nsɛm na onya mmuae fi database no mu.
 
Ɛyɛ akwan a wɔfa so kyerɛ sɛ obi wɔ adwene a ɛyɛ den paa. Mmom sɛ ɛbɛbɔ mmɔden sɛ ɛbɛte nsɛm ase na ɛayɛ mmuae a egyina adesuadeɛ so no, ɛboaboa nsɛm a wɔde kɔ ne nea efi mu ba ano pii sie. Sɛ yɛhwɛ nneyɛe a ɛwɔ hɔ a, ɛsɛ sɛ ɛyɛ nea ɛne ChatGPT yɛ pɛ, sɛ ɛwɔ nneɛma pii a ɛbɛboa a. Eyi ma yɛbisa sɛ, ɛyɛ nokware sɛ adwene ne nteaseɛ hia ankasa na ama yɛatumi ayɛ nkitahodi a ɛwɔ adwene? Wɔ kwan foforo so no: DullGPT yɛ mmuae a ɛne nea nhyehyɛe a ɛwɔ adwene ankasa yɛ no yɛ pɛ, nanso ɛnni nteaseɛ biara anaa adwene a ɛwɔ mu.
 
DullGPT wɔ nneɛma pii a ɛmma ɛnyɛ yiye sɛ ChatGPT, a ɛka ho ne “adesua” no ntɛm ne sɛnea ɛgyina nneɛma foforo a ɛwɔ adwene so, sɛnea ɛnni nkitahodi a atwam ho adwene, ne sɛnea ɛnni nsɛm a wɔkyerɛw (te sɛ Chinese nkyerɛwdeɛ) ho adwene. Mmom no, ɛne nneɛma a ɛwɔ adwene a ɛyɛ kɛse no nsɛ, ɛbɛma mmuae koro pɛ wɔ asɛm koro pɛ so bere nyinaa. Esiane sɛ ɛyɛ adwene ho nhwehwɛmu a ɛnni mfaso biara nti, ɛnni mfaso sɛ yɛbɛbɔ mmɔden sɛ yɛbɛyɛ saa nneɛma yi yiye.
 
Nanso ɛsɛ sɛ yɛka sɛ, meremfa eyi nni nkyerɛ sɛ merebɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛgye Chinese room asɛm no akyi, a ɛbisa sɛ ɛyɛ den sɛ obi bɛnya adwene anaa nteaseɛ ankasa denam nkyerɛwdeɛ a wɔde di dwuma nko ara so. Meka kyerɛ sɛ, ɛwɔ hɔ sɛ nhyehyɛe biako a ɛwɔ adwene a ɛte saa betumi ayɛ, ɛwɔ mu sɛ ɛyɛ kwan a ɛnni mfaso ne nkwaseasɛm so de. Nanso, menyɛ adwene sɛ ɛsɛ sɛ nhyehyɛe biara yɛ nea ɛnni hwee wɔ mu.
 
Hwɛ dwumadie yi ho nkyerɛwdeɛ a efi mu:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import openai
import sqlite3
import string
import itertools
import sys
import os
import pickle

# Nastavení API klíče
OPENAI_API_KEY = "SEM VLOŽ SVŮJ OPENAI API KEY"
openai.api_key = OPENAI_API_KEY

# Cesta k databázi a log souboru
DATABASE_PATH = "dullgpt_db.sqlite"
LOG_FILE = "log.txt"
LAST_INPUT_FILE = "last_input.pkl"

# Funkce pro komunikaci s OpenAI API
def query_chatgpt(prompt):
    response = openai.ChatCompletion.create(
        model="gpt-3.5-turbo",
        messages=[
            {"role": "system", "content": "You are a helpful assistant."},
            {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
        ]
    )
    return response['choices'][0]['message']['content'].strip()

# Funkce pro generování vstupů
def generate_inputs(length):
    characters = string.ascii_letters + string.digits + string.punctuation + " "
    return [''.join(i) for i in itertools.product(characters, repeat=length)]

# Funkce pro vytvoření databáze
def create_db():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS responses (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
            input TEXT NOT NULL,
            output TEXT NOT NULL
        )
    ''')
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro ukládání odpovědí do databáze
def save_response(input_text, output_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        INSERT INTO responses (input, output) VALUES (?, ?)
    ''', (input_text, output_text))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()

# Funkce pro získání odpovědi z databáze
def get_response(input_text):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(DATABASE_PATH)
    c = conn.cursor()
    c.execute('''
        SELECT output FROM responses WHERE input = ?
    ''', (input_text,))
    result = c.fetchone()
    conn.close()
    return result[0] if result else None

# Funkce pro uložení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def save_last_input(input_text):
    with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'wb') as f:
        pickle.dump(input_text, f)

# Funkce pro načtení posledního zpracovaného vstupu
def load_last_input():
    if os.path.exists(LAST_INPUT_FILE):
        with open(LAST_INPUT_FILE, 'rb') as f:
            return pickle.load(f)
    return None

# Funkce pro logování párů vstup-výstup
def log_response(input_text, output_text):
    with open(LOG_FILE, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        f.write(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}\n")

# Režim "učení"
def learn_mode():
    create_db()
    last_input = load_last_input()
    start_length = 1 if not last_input else len(last_input)
    start_index = 0

    if last_input:
        print(f"Loaded last input: '{last_input}'")
        all_inputs = generate_inputs(len(last_input))
        if last_input in all_inputs:
            start_index = all_inputs.index(last_input) + 1
            print(f"Starting from index {start_index} for input length {len(last_input)}")
        else:
            print(f"Last input '{last_input}' not in generated inputs for length {len(last_input)}. Starting from beginning.")
            start_length = len(last_input) + 1
            last_input = None  # Reset last_input to start from the beginning

    while True:  # Loop to continue processing inputs of increasing length
        inputs = generate_inputs(start_length)
        if last_input and start_length == len(last_input):
            start_index = inputs.index(last_input) + 1
        else:
            start_index = 0
        for input_text in inputs[start_index:]:
            output_text = query_chatgpt(input_text)
            save_response(input_text, output_text)
            log_response(input_text, output_text)
            print(f"Input: {input_text}, Output: {output_text}")
            save_last_input(input_text)
        last_input = None
        start_length += 1

# Režim "užívání"
def use_mode():
    create_db()
    while True:
        user_input = input("Enter your input: ")
        output_text = get_response(user_input)
        if output_text:
            print(f"Output: {output_text}")
        else:
            print("No response found for this input.")

# Hlavní část programu
if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ("learn", "use"):
        print("Usage: python dullgpt.py [learn|use]")
        sys.exit(1)
    
    if sys.argv[1] == "learn":
        learn_mode()
    else:
        use_mode()

 Ɔpɛpɔn 2024 
 
 
 
Meyɛɛ nhyehyɛe bi a ɛyɛ mmerɛw a wobetumi de folder baako anaa nea ɛboro saa ama no. Ɛhwehwɛ folder no nyinaa ne nea ɛwɔ ne mu mu, na sɛ ɛhyia mfonini bi (mfonini wɔ ha kyerɛ faayil a ne awiei yɛ .jpg, .jpeg, .png, .tif, .tiff), ɛde ne kwan no kɔka mfonini nyinaa a ɛwɔ hɔ no ho. Sɛ ɛwie hwehwɛ no a, ɛpaw mfonini baako pɛ fi mu na ɛkyerɛ.
 
Afei mekae hard disk nyinaa a me de asie mfonini a meboaboa ano wɔ me nkwa nna nyinaa mu (seesei ɛwɔ mfonini bɛboro 91 000) kɔbɔɔ me kɔmputa no ho. Meyɛɛ hard disk no nyinaa sɛ folder a nhyehyɛe no paw mfonini fi mu. Esiane sɛ ɛho hia nti, na ɛsɛ sɛ mepo folder bi a ɛwɔ mu, efisɛ na ebi wɔ nhyehyɛe no nneɛma nko ara, te sɛ n'akyerɛwde. Sɛ mepira ENTER kwan no so a, nhyehyɛe no paw mfonini a ɛba no ara fi me nkwa nna mu. Sɛ mepira ENTER bio a, nhyehyɛe no paw mfonini foforo, na ɛkɔ so saa...
 
Meyɛɛ series pii a ɛdi akyiri a ɛwɔ mfonini a mepaw no ara mu. Series biara fi ase ne mfonini a mepaw no ara, na ɛkɔ so paw mfonini foforo kosi sɛ ɛbɛhyia nea mempɛ sɛ meyi no adi. Mfonini a etwa to yi na wɔkyerɛ no wɔ series no mu a ɛyɛ dɛm. Na mepɛ sɛ minim mfonini bɛn na ɛma series no twa mu na adɛn nti. Mebɛhuu sɛ, nea ɛyɛ nwonwa no, nea ɛma ɛtwa mu ne sɛ na me suro sɛ mebɛyi afoforo ankorankoro asɛm adi. Nneɛma foforo a ɛma ɛtwa mu ne adwuma mu nkrataa, afoforo afie, ne me ankasa fie, ne nea ɛkeka ho.
 
Saa dwumadi yi maa mehuu sɛnea me ankorankoro asɛm hyehyɛe no yɛ aduane, sɛ ɛwɔ hɔ koraa a. Nanso, ɛduruu bere bi a na ɛsɛ sɛ mepaw sɛ meyi mfonini no adi anaa meanyi no adi. Nanso, saa apaw yi yɛ den na ɛnyɛ nea ɛda hɔ fann.

 Python source code a ɛwɔ dwumadi yi ho no wɔ ha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import os
import random
from pathlib import Path

def open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths):
    # Převod cest na absolutní cesty
    folder_paths = [Path(folder).resolve() for folder in folder_paths]
    excluded_paths = [Path(excluded).resolve() for excluded in excluded_paths]

    # Seznam podporovaných přípon souborů
    supported_extensions = ['.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', 'tif', 'tiff']

    # Seznam pro ukládání cest k obrázkům
    images = []

    # Prochází všechny zadané adresáře
    for folder_path in folder_paths:
        # Prochází soubory v adresáři a jeho podsložkách
        for path in folder_path.rglob('*'):
            # Přidává cesty k obrázkům, pokud mají podporovanou příponu
            if path.suffix.lower() in supported_extensions:
                images.append(path)

    while True:
        if not images:
            print("V zadaných adresářích nebyly nalezeny žádné obrázky.")
            return

        # Náhodný výběr souboru
        image_path = random.choice(images)

        # Kontroluje, zda vybraná cesta neleží v některém z vyloučených adresářů
        if any(image_path.is_relative_to(excluded) for excluded in excluded_paths):
            continue  # Pokud je cesta vyloučená, vybere jiný obrázek

        print(f"Otevírám obrázek: {image_path}")
        os.startfile(image_path)
        return

# Seznam cest k adresářům
folder_paths = [
    r"D:\\02_záloha dat",
    r"G:",
    r"K:"
    #r"C:"
]

# Seznam cest k vyloučení
excluded_paths = [
    r"K:\\WEB",
]

while True:  # Nekonečná smyčka
    open_random_image(folder_paths, excluded_paths)
    input("Stiskni ENTER pro opětovné spuštění nebo Ctrl+C pro ukončení programu...")

[/CODE BLOCK]

Wave functions
 
 Ɔpɛnimaa 2024 
 
 
 
Adwuma yi yɛ nhwɛsoɔ a ɛkyerɛ sɛnea yebetumi asusuw nsuo anaa mframa nnyigyeɛ a ɛtrɛ na ɛbɔ ntam, ne sɛnea ɛne nneɛma a atwa yɛn ho ahyia di nkitaho, denam akontaabu nhyehyɛeɛ ne kɔmputa so nhwɛsoɔ so. Sɛ yɛde nneɛma a ɛba kwan so di dwuma ma ɛkyerɛ baabi a nnyigyeɛ no fi, ne dodoɔ ne ne kɛseɛ, ne sɛnea yɛde nnyigyeɛ a ɛteɛ ase bere a ɛrekɔ akyirikyiri no di dwuma, ne sɛnea nnyigyeɛ no bɔ ntam wɔ kwan no ano, ma ɛyɛ mfonini a ɛyɛ anigye a ɛkyerɛ sɛnea nnyigyeɛ nkitahodie yɛ den wɔ baabi a ɛhyeɛ wɔ. Wɔde kɔla a ɛyɛ tuntum ne fitaa na ɛkyerɛ mfonini no, a ɛkyerɛ nnyigyeɛ no ahoɔden wɔ baabi a ɛwɔ kwan no mu.

Source code
 Python source code ma adwuma yi wɔ ha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import os
import re

# Počet obrázků k vytvoření
num_images = 1

# Nastavení rozsahů pro náhodné hodnoty
frequency_range = (1, 50)  # Rozsah frekvencí vln
amplitude_range = (0.1, 1)  # Rozsah amplitud vln

# Počet zdrojů
number_of_sources = 3

# Velikost prostoru pro simulaci a rozlišení mřížky
space_size = 5
grid_resolution = 1000

for i in range(num_images):
    # Inicializace pole pro simulaci
    Z = np.zeros((grid_resolution, grid_resolution))

    # Vytvoření mřížky pro simulaci
    x = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    y = np.linspace(-space_size, space_size, grid_resolution)
    X, Y = np.meshgrid(x, y)

    # Generování náhodných zdrojů
    for _ in range(number_of_sources):
        source_position = np.random.uniform(-space_size, space_size, 2)
        frequency = np.random.uniform(*frequency_range)
        amplitude = np.random.uniform(*amplitude_range)

        # Výpočet vzdálenosti od zdroje a přidání vlny do simulace
        R = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
        Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R) / (R + 1)  # Přidání útlumu amplitudy

        # Odrážení vln od stěn
        for wall in [-space_size, space_size]:
            R_vertical = np.sqrt((X - 2*wall + source_position[0])**2 + (Y - source_position[1])**2)
            R_horizontal = np.sqrt((X - source_position[0])**2 + (Y - 2*wall + source_position[1])**2)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_vertical) / (R_vertical + 1)
            Z += amplitude * np.sin(frequency * R_horizontal) / (R_horizontal + 1)

    # Vykreslení výsledku
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 10))
    plt.imshow(Z, cmap='gray', extent=[-space_size, space_size, -space_size, space_size])
    plt.axis('off')
    
    # Před uložením obrázku použijte tight_layout pro optimalizaci rozložení
    #plt.tight_layout()

    # Uložení obrázku
    pattern = r"wave_interference_pattern_(\d+).png"
    existing_files = [f for f in os.listdir('.') if re.match(pattern, f)]
    if existing_files:
        numbers = [int(re.search(pattern, f).group(1)) for f in existing_files]
        max_number = max(numbers)
    else:
        max_number = 0
    file_name = f"wave_interference_pattern_{max_number + 1:05d}.png"
    plt.savefig(file_name, bbox_inches='tight', pad_inches=0, transparent=False, dpi=(grid_resolution*1.3)//10)
    plt.close()  # Zavření obrázku po uložení

[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Cursor trajectory
SLUG: trajektorie-kurzoru

LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs

============================================================
Cursor trajectory
 
 August 2023 
 
 
 
Ebetumi ayɛ sɛ kwan a yɛfa so di dwuma da biara ne kɔmputa mouse yɛ ade a ɛyɛ adwuma a ɛnni hwee a ɛyɛ nwanwa. Nanso dɛn na ɛbɛba sɛ yɛbɛtumi asesa saa nneyɛe a yɛyɛ da biara yi ayɛ no ade foforo, ade a ɛma yɛnya kwan a yɛfa so hu kɔmputa adwuma wɔ kwan a ɛyɛ nwanwa so? Esiane koodu a ɛkyerɛ baabi a mouse cursor no wɔ ne kɔla a ɛwɔ ne ase nti, saa adwene yi betumi ayɛ nokware.
 
Ɛyɛ adwuma dɛn?
 Saa koodu yi di baabi a mouse cursor no wɔ wɔ screen no so ne kɔla a ɛwɔ pixel a ɛwɔ ne ase no akyi. Wɔde saa nsɛm yi kɔma virtual canvas a ɛwɔ resolution a ɛne screen no resolution yɛ pɛ wɔ bere a ɛyɛ pɛ no ara mu. Bere biara a wode mouse no bɛyɛ adwuma no, koodu no bɛkyerɛ pixel a ɛwɔ kɔla a na ɛwɔ cursor no ase no wɔ baabi a cursor no wɔ. Eyi kyerɛ sɛ bere a woredi dwuma wɔ screen no so na woreyɛ nneɛma no, kɔla ahorow a ɛkyerɛ kwan a wofa so yɛ adwuma no bɛba canvas no so. Saa kwan ahorow yi yɛ nea ɛyɛ soronko na ɛkyerɛ sɛnea obi di dwuma ne kɔmputa.
 
Dɛn na ɛbɛba sɛ ɛkɔ so bere tenten?
 Sɛ saa adwuma yi kɔ so bere tenten a, canvas no bɛhyɛ ma na kwan ahorow a wofa so yɛ adwuma no bɛyɛ nea ɛnyɛ den sɛ wubehu. Mmom no, canvas no fã kɛse no ara bɛyɛ nea ɛte sɛ nneɛma a ɛnyɛ nhyehyɛe. Nanso, screen no so fã bi wɔ hɔ a ɛnsesa pii bere a ɛkɔ so no. Ebetumi ayɛ Windows system bar a ɛwɔ ase no, baabi a software icons ahorow wɔ. Saa fã ahorow yi wɔ canvas no so no kɔ so yɛ nea ɛte saa ara.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-1.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-2.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-3.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-4.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-5.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-6.png] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/trajektorie-kurzoru-obr-7.png] 
 
Awiei
 Saa koodu yi yɛ ntease a ɛwɔ mfiridwuma ne adwinni ntam, a ɛma yɛtumi sesa kwan a yɛfa so di dwuma da biara ne kɔmputa yɛ no ade a ɛyɛ fɛ. Ɛwom sɛ ɛte sɛ nea kɔmputa adwuma yɛ ade a ɛyɛ da biara nanso saa adwuma yi kyerɛ sɛ saa nneyɛe yi nso betumi anya ne ho fɛ ne ne nnyigyei.
 
Source code
 Python source code a ɛfa saa adwuma yi ho wɔ ha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
 import pygame
import pyautogui
import sys
import time

# Inicializace Pygame
pygame.init()

# Získání rozměrů obrazovky
screen_info = pygame.display.Info()
screen_width = screen_info.current_w
screen_height = screen_info.current_h

# Vytvoření okna o rozměrech shodných s rozměry obrazovky
screen = pygame.display.set_mode((screen_width, screen_height))

# Hlavní smyčka programu
running = True
start_time = time.time()
while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            running = False
        elif event.type == pygame.KEYDOWN:
            if event.key == pygame.K_ESCAPE:  # Když stisknete klávesu ESC, okno se zavře
                running = False

    # Získání souřadnic kurzoru pomocí PyAutoGUI
    x, y = pyautogui.position()

    # Získání barvy pixelu pod kurzorem
    if 0 <= x < screen_width and 0 <= y < screen_height:  # Zkontrolujte, zda jsou souřadnice v rámci obrazovky
        try:
            r, g, b = pyautogui.pixel(x, y)
            # Nabarvení pixelu na plátně touto barvou
            screen.set_at((x, y), (r, g, b))
        except:
            pass

    # Aktualizace zobrazení
    pygame.display.flip()

    # Uložení záložního obrázku každé 3 minuty
    if time.time() - start_time >= 180:  # 180 sekund = 3 minuty
        pygame.image.save(screen, "output_backup.png")
        start_time = time.time()

# Uložení obrázku
pygame.image.save(screen, "output.png")

# Ukončení Pygame
pygame.quit()
sys.exit()
[/CODE BLOCK]

TITLE: Nnwuma a ɛfa nnua ho
SLUG: zaznamy-rostlin
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/zaznamy-rostlin/

BUILT_HTML: zaznamy-rostlin/index.html
============================================================
Nnwuma a ɛfa nnua ho
 
 ebɔ 2023 
 
 
 
Nnianim nsɛm
 Wɔ saa kyerɛw yi mu no, yɛbɛhwɛ dwumadie bi a ɛka nnua, nneɛma a ɛfa eletriki, nhyehyɛe a wɔde yɛ nneɛma (programming) ne adwinne a wɔde ani hu bom. Dwumadie yi de Arduino ne Python di dwuma de yɛ mfonini a ɛkyerɛ sɛnea nnua te nka wɔ wɔn asetra mu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png | arduino nhyehyɛe a wɔde akyekyere]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-1.png] 
 
Ɛyɛ adwuma dɛn?
 Arduino no wɔ nneɛma mmiɛnsa a ɛkyerɛ nsakrae (sensors) – nea ɛkyerɛ hyeɛ, nea ɛkyerɛ hann, ne nea ɛkyerɛ nsuo dodoɔ wɔ asase mu. Wɔde saa nneɛma yi ahyɛ kuku a nnua ahorow (te sɛ patizon, kookoo, anwummere hann, nkatabo, ne afoforo) wom mu. Saa nneɛma yi boaboa nneɛma ano daa fa hyeɛ, hann, ne nsuo a ɛwɔ asase mu ho.
 Nhyehyɛe no bɛyɛ kwan a ɛyɛ fɛ a ne kɛse yɛ 100×100 pixel. Wɔde hann a ɛfiri nneɛma a ɛkyerɛ nsakrae no mu na ɛsiesie pixel biara wɔ kwan no so. RGB kɔla a pixel no wɔ no gyina nneɛma a wɔanya no so: R (kɔkɔɔ) kyerɛ hyeɛ, G (ahahanam) kyerɛ hann, na B (bruu) kyerɛ nsuo.
 
Da koro mfonini
 Nea edi kan no, yɛbɛhwɛ dwumadie no fã a ɛyɛ mmerɛw, baabi a mfonini koro gyina hɔ ma da koro wɔ dua no asetra mu. Nhyehyɛe no hyɛ aseɛ denam pixel a edi kan a ɛwɔ benkum so atifi no a wɔde kɔla bɛhyɛ mu, na ɛkɔ so yɛ saa ma pixel biara gyina nneɛma a wɔanya no so. Sɛ wɔde kɔla hyɛ pixel no mu wie a, nhyehyɛe no twɛn kakra ansa na akɔ pixel a edi hɔ no so. Sɛ ɛdu adaka no awiei a, ɛkɔ adaka a edi hɔ no mfiase na ɛkɔ so yɛ saa. Saa adwuma yi kɔ so kosi sɛ wɔde kɔla bɛhyɛ pixel nyinaa mu.
 
Anwummere hann ho nkrataa:
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-2.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-3.png] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-4.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-4.png]

Nneɛma a wɔabom ayɛ
 Sɛ yɛwie da koro mfonini no a, yɛbɛtumi ayɛ nneɛma pii a ɛkyerɛ bere tenten.
 Wɔbɛtumi de mfonini nketenkete no ato wɔn ho so, na ama ayɛ mfonini kɛse. Esiane sɛ sɛ wɔwie mfonini koro a, ɛfiri nea ɛdi hɔ no ase ntɛm ara nti, bere no ankasa nnyae. Saa kwan yi so no, yɛbɛtumi ayɛ mfonini a ɛwɔ 100x500 pixel, sɛ yɛde mfonini nketenkete nnum abom a.
 
Anwummere hann ho nkrataa:
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-9.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-10.png]

Patizon ho nkrataa:

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-11.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-12.png] 
 
 
Bere a ɛda nneɛma a wɔkyerɛ no ntam no a wɔbɛtrɛw mu
 Wɔbɛtumi atrɛw bere a ɛda pixel biara a wɔkyerɛ no ntam no mu. Saa kwan yi so no, kwan koro a ɛyɛ 100×100 pixel no bɛtumi agyina hɔ ama nna pii, ɛnyɛ da koro pɛ. Saa nhyehyɛe yi ma yɛbɛtumi ahwɛ sɛnea dua no renyin wɔ bere tenten mu wɔ mfonini koro pɛ so.
 
Anwummere hann ho nkrataa:

Nnyinasosɛm a wɔabom
 Sɛ yɛbom de nnyinasosɛm mmienu no di dwuma a, yɛbɛtumi ayɛ mfonini pii, baabi a mfonini biara gyina hɔ ma nna pii, na mfonini no nyinaa bom kyerɛ nna pii anaa dapɛn pii. Saa kwan yi so no, yɛbɛtumi ahwɛ na yɛayɛ mfonini a ɛkyerɛ sɛnea dua no renyin wɔ bere tenten mu.
 
Anwummere hann ho nkrataa:
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-15.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-15.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-16.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-16.png] 
 
Anwummere hann ho nkrataa:
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-17.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-17.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-18.png]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zaznamy-rostlin-obr-18.png] 
 
 
Nneɛma a wɔanya no asekyerɛ
 Mfonini a yɛanya no ma yɛn kwan soronko a yɛfa so hu nnua asetra. “Akyerɛw” bruu a ɛwɔ mfonini no so no kyerɛ anadwo, efisɛ na nsuo wɔ hɔ, nanso na hyeɛ nni hɔ dodo, na hann nso nni hɔ. Opia no, “akyerɛw” kɔkɔɔ no ba efisɛ da no awiei no, owia hyerɛn dua no so, na ɛmaa hyeɛ ne hann dodoɔ kɔɔ soro. Eyi maa mfonini no mu kɔkɔɔ ne ahahanam kɔla no dɔɔ, na ɛno na ɛmaa kɔla kɔkɔɔ no bae. Ɛtɔ da bi a, anadwo mfiase no, yɛhu akyerɛw nketenkete, efisɛ na mehyɛ kanea wɔ me dan mu anwummere. Wɔ mfonini afoforo so no, yɛbɛtumi ahu hann a ɛso teɛ no sɛ ɛkyerɛ sɛ na ɛresuo.
 
Nhyehyɛe kood (Source code)
 Python kood a wɔde yɛɛ dwumadie yi wɔ ha:
 
[CODE BLOCK]
import serial
import pygame
import pygame.image
import decimal
import datetime

# Define constants
MAX_8BIT = 255
MAX_10BIT = 1023
WIDTH = 100
HEIGHT = WIDTH
SERIAL_PORT = 'COM3'
DELAY = 20 * 1000
MAX_RETRIES = 5
MIN_HUMIDITY = 750
MAX_HUMIDITY = 850

def actual_time():
    now_raw = str(datetime.datetime.now())
    now = now_raw.replace(":", "-")
    return now

def temperature_to_interval(temperature):
    return 1 / (1 + 2**(-1 * (temperature - 22)))

def remap_humidity(humidity, min_measured, max_measured):
    humidity_range = max_measured - min_measured
    humidity_proportion = (humidity - min_measured) / humidity_range
    lightness = int(humidity_proportion * MAX_10BIT)
    return min(max(lightness, 0), MAX_10BIT)

def max_255(v):
    return min(v, MAX_8BIT)

def remap_10bit_to_8bit(number):
    if not 0 <= number <= MAX_10BIT:
        raise ValueError("Input number must be between 0 and 1023")
    return round(number / 4)

def separate_data(data):
    temp, light, hum = map(float, data.split("|"))
    return temp, light, hum

def write_to_log(log):
    with open('log.txt', 'a') as f:
        f.write(log + "\n")

def read_arduino_data(ser, delay):
    data1 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data2 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    data3 = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(delay)
    if len(data1) != len(data2) or len(data2) != len(data3):
        return read_arduino_data(ser, delay)
    return data1, data2, data3

ser = serial.Serial(port=SERIAL_PORT, baudrate=115200)

pygame.init()
win = pygame.display.set_mode((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
surface = pygame.Surface((WIDTH, HEIGHT))
pygame.display.set_caption("TEMPERATURE + LIGHT + HUMIDITY")

for i in range(5):
    data_test = ser.readline().decode().strip()
    pygame.time.wait(100)

x = 0
y = 0
img_num = 0
sensitivity = 1
start_time = actual_time()
running = True

while running:
    for event in pygame.event.get():
        if event.type == pygame.QUIT:
            finish_time = actual_time()
            pygame.display.update()
            name = str(img_num)
            pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {} (incomplete).png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
            print("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            write_to_log("IMAGE SAVED BUT INCOMPLETE")
            running = False
    if running == False:
        break

    for _ in range(MAX_RETRIES):
        try:
            data1, data2, data3 = read_arduino_data(ser, DELAY)
            break
        except Exception:
            print("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            write_to_log("COULD NOT READ THE DATA, TRYING AGAIN...")
            pygame.time.wait(500)
    else:
        print("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        write_to_log("FAILED TO READ DATA AFTER {} RETRIES".format(MAX_RETRIES))
        continue

    Rraw = (separate_data(data1)[0] + separate_data(data2)[0] + separate_data(data3)[0]) / 3
    R = round(max_255(temperature_to_interval(Rraw) * MAX_8BIT * sensitivity))

    Graw = (separate_data(data1)[1] + separate_data(data2)[1] + separate_data(data3)[1]) / 3
    G = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(Graw) * sensitivity))

    Braw = (separate_data(data1)[2] + separate_data(data2)[2] + separate_data(data1)[2]) / 3
    B = remap_humidity(Braw, min_measured=MIN_HUMIDITY, max_measured=MAX_HUMIDITY)
    B = round(max_255(remap_10bit_to_8bit(B) * sensitivity))

    color = [R, G, B]
    pygame.draw.rect(win, color, [x, y, 1, 1])
    surface.set_at((x, y), (color[0], color[1], color[2]))
    if (x + 1) < WIDTH:
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 255, 0], [x + 1, y, 1, 1])
    pygame.display.flip()

    #   print current data with colored text
    if color[0] + color[1] + color[2] > 127:
        text_color = "0"
    else:
        text_color = "255"
    log = "COLOR: [{}, {}, {}],  TEMP.: {:.2f}°C, LIGHT: {}, HUM.: {},  TIME: {},  IMAGE NUMBER: {},   COMPLETED: {:.3f}%,  x: {}, y: {}" \
        .format(R, G, B, Rraw, round(Graw), round(Braw), actual_time(), img_num, (WIDTH * y + x + 1) / (WIDTH * HEIGHT) * 100, x, y)
    print('\033[38;2;{};{};{}m\033[48;2;{};{};{}m'
          .format(text_color, text_color, text_color, str(color[0]), str(color[1]), str(color[2])) +
          log +
          '\033[0m')
    write_to_log(log)

    x += 1
    if x >= WIDTH:
        x = 0
        y += 1
    if y >= HEIGHT:
        y = 0
        finish_time = actual_time()
        pygame.display.update()
        name = str(img_num)
        pygame.image.save(surface, '{}, START {}, FINISH {}.png'.format(name, start_time, finish_time))
        print("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        write_to_log("IMAGE SUCCESSFULLY SAVED")
        pygame.draw.rect(win, [0, 0, 0], [0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT])
        surface.fill((0, 0, 0))
        img_num += 1
        start_time = actual_time()
[/CODE BLOCK]
 
 
Awieiɛ
 Dwumadie yi yɛ nhwɛsoɔ pa a ɛkyerɛ sɛnea yɛbɛtumi de tekinoloji adi dwuma de ate nneɛma a ɛwɔ abɔdeɛ mu ase yiye. Mfonini a yɛanya no ma yɛn kwan a yɛfa so hu nnua asetra ne nneɛma a ɛma wɔn ho yɛ wɔn.
 
Ɛho hia sɛ yɛhu sɛ, ɛwom sɛ dwumadie yi ma yɛn kwan a yɛfa so hu nnua asetra de, nanso ɛnka nneɛma nyinaa a nnua te nka no ho. Nnua tumi te nneɛma pii a ɛwɔ wɔn ho ho nka, a ɛka nsuo a ɛwɔ mframa mu, owia kwan, asase mu nneɛma, eletriki ne nneɛma a ɛma wɔn ho yɛ wɔn. Dwumadie yi nni botae sɛ ɛbɛkyerɛ nnua asetra nyinaa, na mmom ɛhwɛ nneɛma mmiɛnsa a ɛho hia a yɛbɛtumi akyerɛw na yɛayɛ ho mfonini.
 
Yennim sɛnea nnua no kyerɛ saa nneɛma yi ase, nanso yennim sɛ wɔyɛ ho biribi. Dwumadie yi yɛ bɔmmɔdenbɔ a yɛde rekyerɛ saa nneɛma yi ase kɔ kwan a yɛbɛtumi ate ase yiye.

TITLE: Recursion wɔ MS Word nhyehyɛe no mu
SLUG: rekurze-programu-ms-word
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/rekurze-programu-ms-word/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-11-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/rekurze-programu-ms-word.mdx
BUILT_HTML: rekurze-programu-ms-word/index.html
============================================================
Recursion wɔ MS Word nhyehyɛe no mu
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2022 
 
 
 
Mɛhu kwan a yebetumi de ahyɛ recursive process ase wɔ Microsoft Word nhyehyɛe no mu. Ɛhia nneɛma mmienu de ayɛ saa – link function ne table. Sɛ wode link function no di dwuma a, wubetumi de document A mu nneɛma ahyɛ document B mu, na sɛ wosesa nneɛma a ɛwɔ document A mu a, ɛbɛsesa wɔ document B mu nso. Sɛ yɛka no foforo a – document B mu nneɛma no kyerɛ document A mu nneɛma. Yebetumi de recursion no ahyɛ ase denam nneɛma a ɛkyerɛ ne ho so. Dɛn na yebetumi ayɛ de adu saa? Ɛsɛ sɛ woyɛ document A (ma yɛnfrɛ no sɛ nhwɛsoɔ doc A.docx) ne document B (doc B.docx), na woyɛ table a ɛyɛ kwa wɔ document A mu, na woyiyi no na wode link no ahyɛ document B mu. Sɛ woyiyi table foforo a wode ahyɛ mu no fi document B mu na wode link no ahyɛ table a ɛwɔ document A mu a, ɛno na ɛyɛ. Eyi bɛma table a ɛhyɛ ne ho mu abɔ ase. Daa no, MS Word bɛhyɛ aseɛ sɛ ɛrekyere na awiei koraa no ɛbɛtɔ ase. Wubetumi ahu saa kwan yi nyinaa wɔ video a ɛwɔ aseɛ ha no mu.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/xaE-eYQw4Mw]

TITLE: Anwensem
SLUG: pseudobasen
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/pseudobasen/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2022-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/pseudobasen.mdx
BUILT_HTML: pseudobasen/index.html
============================================================
Anwensem
 
 Kotonima 2022 
 
 
 
Nsɛmfua nyinaa a ɛwɔ anwensem a edi hɔ yi mu (sɛ yɛka sɛ ɛyɛ yɛn adwene na yɛyi nsɛmfua a ɛboa ma kasa no kɔ so no fi hɔ a) yɛ nsɛmfua a ɛnni ase; ɛno ne sɛ, ɛyɛ nsɛmfua a anka ɛbɛtumi abɛyɛ nokware wɔ Czech kasa nnyigyei mu – nsɛmfua a ɛte saa no nnyigyei nkabom no yɛ ade a ɛwɔ kasa no mu – nanso wonni ase biara na wɔmfa nni dwuma. Enti anwensem no nni ase koraa, na sɛ obi pɛ sɛ ɔkyerɛ ase a, ɛgyina akenkanfo no adwene so, ɛwom sɛ nsɛmfua no bi nnyigyei ma ɛyɛ anigye de.
 
Eyi nyɛ adwuma a wɔyɛe sɛ wɔde bɛyɛ anwensem a ɛwɔ anuonyam, mmom ɛyɛ bɔɔl a wɔbɔe de hwɛ kasa no tumi. Ɛyɛ nwanwa sɛ, nsɛmfua a ɛnni ase yi nyinaa mu no, wobɛtumi ahu nea ɛyɛ din, adeyɛ, anaa nea ɛkyerɛ nneɛma nnyinaso ne nea ɛwɔ mu.
  
JEZÁPEK

V Městnale se krůcky škvoury
 K mochci ďáry vyněraly
 Pulstočmý kroch zanářel
 Do stupí honkaje vořenky
 Když jezápek kokeřil pod husečným vachalem koltěním zmán

Hlůmajíce zlanivou vostrčlici
 Sníkali garánci garánkám vrůzy
 A lačili jezápka zastrožlým přímanem
 Zastrožlým z blímného vesepství
 Dokřmeným lipačskou vrýnou

Hrů hrů! kafolčil jezápek zpufněle
 A holivěl likajky garánkám
 Až z hlemuňků kriskaly baťafky
 A cuchraly zrepčeným blizalkám
 Jalvičné zromky
  
Ɛwom sɛ anwensem no nni ase de, nanso bere a obi rekenkan no, ɛbɛtumi ama adwene bi aba ne tirim. Yɛpɛɛ sɛ yɛhu nea adwene a ɛwɔ nneɛma a ɛyɛ adwene (AI) bɛka ho. Yɛde AI nhyehyɛe ahorow bi dii dwuma (ru-DALL-E, Dream, Deep Daze, starryai – Altair, starryai – Orion ne Night Café), a ɛtumi de nsɛm yɛ mfonini. Nhyehyɛe biara yɛ soronko, ebinom yɛ mfonini a ɛte sɛ nea ɛwɔ nokware mu, ebinom nso yɛ nea ɛyɛ adwene, na ebinom nso yɛ nea wɔde nneyɛe ahorow ayɛ. Wɔn nso nte Czech kasa no ase pii (sɛ nhwɛso no, ru-DALL-E te Czech kasa ase yiye).
 
Yɛde anwensem no fã ahorow no maa AI nhyehyɛe ahorow no, na nhyehyɛe biara yɛɛ ne mfonini a ɛyɛ soronko a ɛgyina nea ɛte ase wɔ fã no mu. Yɛde mfonini ahorow no yɛɛ video, baabi a mfonini no kɔ so na wɔrekenkan anwensem no fã no. Deep Daze nhyehyɛe no tumi kyerɛ sɛnea mfonini no reba, enti wubetumi ahu sɛnea mfonini no renyin na ɛreyɛ yiye wɔ video no mu.
 
ruDALL-E
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/CniEMECxcgw]
 
 
Dream
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/MIS05zpqcWA]
 
 
starryai (Altair)
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/qsmicN46yBk]
 
 
starryai (Orion)
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/JDU4Zi6D9rE]
 
 
Night Café
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/txD8apt8CJo]
 
 
Deep Daze
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/m06W3aQXKUs]
 
 
Dɛn na anwensem no ma woadwen ho? Wo adwene a ɛwɔ anwensem no ho ne nea AI kyerɛ no hyia?
 
anwensem no kyerɛwfo:
 Filip Drábek
 
mfonini no kyerɛwfo:
 Vojta Maur

TITLE: Me ho ho mfon (adwuma a yɛde wie sukuu)
SLUG: autoportret-prakticka-maturitni-prace

TITLE: Aboterɛ a ɛfa asase so nkwa ho
SLUG: strucna-historie-zivota-zemi
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/strucna-historie-zivota-zemi/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2021-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/strucna-historie-zivota-zemi.mdx
BUILT_HTML: strucna-historie-zivota-zemi/index.html
============================================================
Aboterɛ a ɛfa asase so nkwa ho
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2021 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/1R8M4nRLPWQ]
 
 
Wɔyɛɛ video yi sɛ sukuu adwuma wɔ Střední odborná škola obchodu, užitého umění a designu a Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Video yi mfonini koro gyina hɔ ma 200 000 mfeɛ a asase wɔ hɔ. Eyi bɛyɛ te sɛ bere a Homo sapiens nkurɔfoɔ no de tenaa asase so. Ɛno nti na wɔkyerɛɛ onipa no wɔ video no mfonini a etwa to koraa no mu.
 
Mfonini nyinaa a wɔde dii dwuma wɔ video no mu no, ɔkyerɛwfoɔ no ara na ɔkyeree.

============================================================
TITLE: Asetena a Hromnické jezírko
SLUG: zivot-hromnickem-jezirku
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2021-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku.mdx
BUILT_HTML: zivot-hromnickem-jezirku/index.html
============================================================
Asetena a Hromnické jezírko
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2021 
 
 
 

[MediaObject: https://www.google.com/maps/embed?pb=!1m18!1m12!1m3!1d2873.387394621076!2d13.444731496763541!3d49.85061064085366!2m3!1f0!2f0!3f0!3m2!1i1024!2i768!4f13.1!3m3!1m2!1s0x0%3A0x93e249730f9d76f5!2zSHJvbW5pY2vDqSBqZXrDrXJrbw!5e1!3m2!1scs!2scz!4v1609929799218!5m2!1scs!2scz]

 
Hromnické jezírko [https://cs.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hromnick%C3%A9_jez%C3%ADrko] yɛ beaeɛ bi a wɔbɔ ho ban wɔ Plzeň-sever mantam mu. Esiane dɛ wɔtwaa aboɔ wɔ hɔ tete nti, nsuo a ɛwɔ hɔ no yɛ sulfuric acid a ɛnyɛ den (ne pH yɛ 2.6 – 2.9). Ɛno nti na wɔkyerɛw wɔ nkrataa pii so dɛ "nsuo no yɛ steril na nkwa nni mu". Mpo wɔ adwumayɛbea krataa a ɛkyerɛkyerɛ nneɛma mu [https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/d/d0/Tabule_Hromnick%C3%A9_jez%C3%ADrko.JPG] no, wɔakyerɛw dɛ: "...gye dɛ nsuo no mu nsuo-nnuaba (algae) nko ara na nkwa nni mu". Saa adwene yi amfa me ho, efisɛ nkwa tumi sesa ne ho yiye. Enti megyee gyinae dɛ mɛkɔ hɔ akɔhwɛ me ankasa.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-1.jpg | Hromnické jezírko]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-2.jpg | nsuo no nhwehwɛmu pH]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-2.jpg] 
 
19. Ɔpɛnimaa 2020 no, mefaa nsuo bi fii Hromnické jezírko. Ná nkwa a ɛwɔ mu no da adi bere a nsuo no ho dɔteɛ no tɔeɛ no. Mihuu pakomár mmoa nketenkete bi wɔ mu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-3.jpg | pakomár mmoa ]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-3.jpg] 
 
Mihuu mmoa bi nso a ɛte sɛ mmoa a wɔwɔ ntaban, na me de microscope na mehuu ne su.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-4.jpg | mmoa a wɔwɔ ntaban ]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-4.jpg] 
 
Mihuu mmoa nketenkete a ɛwɔ nseɛ baako pɛ, te sɛ krásnoočka ahorow mmienu yi, na na wɔdɔɔso araa ma wɔyɛɛ kɔla kɔkɔɔ wɔ nsuo no ani a wobetumi de w'ani ahu.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-5.jpg | krásnoočka ]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-5.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-6.jpg | krásnoočka ]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-6.jpg] 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/aoPDRJ_XaxI]
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-7.jpg | krásnoočko ]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-7.jpg] 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/XbHSxjzpSYE]
 
 
Wobetumi ahu mmoa nketenkete a wɔn ho nni nkyekyɛmu (prokaryotic) wɔ mfonini a ɛwɔ soro hɔ – sinice (bakteria nketenkete a wɔn su te sɛ dua). Bakteria foforo a mihuu wɔ nsuo no mu ne yi (a mennim ne din pɔtee mprempren).
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-8.jpg | bakteria ]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-8.jpg] 
 
Bere a mehwɛɛ nsuo no bɛyɛ nnawɔtwe mmienu akyi no, mihuu mmoa bi a wɔfrɛ wɔn nálevníky. Nea edi kan ne vířenka, na nea ɛtɔ so mmienu ne uroleptus.
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-9.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-9.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-10.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zivot-hromnickem-jezirku-obr-10.jpg] 
 
 
Ɛyɛ nwanwa dɛ mmoa dɔɔso saa wɔ nsuo a ɛyɛ acid dodo saa mu. Enti me adwene ne dɛ, nsuo no yɛ steril no nyɛ nokware. Ɛsɛ dɛ wɔsesa nkrataa a ɛwɔ hɔ no ne intanɛt so nkrataa a wɔakyerɛw saa asɛm no. Nanso, eyinom nnyɛ mmoa a wɔwɔ Hromnické jezírko nyinaa, enti ɛbɛyɛ papa dɛ yɛbɛyɛ nhwehwɛmu foforo wɔ afe no bere ahorow mu, anaa yɛbɛfa nsuo wɔ nsuo no ase hɔ (efisɛ ɛhɔ no, oxygen ne hann a ɛnni hɔ nti, bakteria a wɔnhia oxygen betumi atra hɔ).

Adwene a ɛfa adwene a ɛkyerɛ nneɛma a ɛnni hɔ ho
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2020 
 
 
 
Wɔkyerɛw saa nkyerɛwee yi sɛ sukuu adwuma (suban: adwene a ɛfa nneɛma ho) wɔ nsɛm biara a wopɛ ho bere a na mepɛ adesua wɔ Střední odborné škole obchodu, užitého umění a designu a ɛwɔ Plzeň.
