Onipa suban ho mfonini
 
 Ɔpɛnimaa 2018 
 
 
 
Wɔyɛɛ saa dwumadie yi sɛ sukuu adwuma ma Fotografie adesua wɔ Střední odborná škola obchodu, užitého umění a designu a ɛwɔ Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Botaeɛ no ne sɛ wɔbɛka mfonini a wɔde kyerɛ onipa suban, onipa suban ho adwene, mfonini a wɔde kyerɛ, animasi ne nnyigyeɛ abom wɔ akwan a ɛyɛ nwonwa so. Mfonini ne video a efi mu baeɛ no gyinaa Eysenck onipa suban ho nhwehwɛmu ne Galen nteaseɛ a ɛfa nipa suban anan ho so.

TITLE: Checkerboard raster
SLUG: sachovnicovy-rastr
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/sachovnicovy-rastr/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-12-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/sachovnicovy-rastr.mdx
BUILT_HTML: sachovnicovy-rastr/index.html
============================================================
Checkerboard raster
 
 Ɔpɛnimaa 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/lD_PCIbCvlk]
 
 
Mede checkerboard raster no ahyɛ mfonini a ɛyɛ nhwɛsoɔ no so, baabi a checkerboard square biara yɛ pixel baako wɔ mfonini no mu. Checkerboard square no fitaa no yɛ mfonini no pixel a ɛwɔ hɔ dedaw, na nea ɛyɛ tuntum no nso yɛ ne kɔla ne ne hann a wɔakyerɛ no wɔ kwan foforɔ so. Mfonini a ɛyɛ kɛse no yɛ sɛ ɛyɛ kɔla kɔkɔɔ bi, nanso sɛ wode digital zoom kɔ mu a, na woahu mfonini ankasa no. Sɛ wopɛ sɛ ɛda adi yie wɔ Full HD monitor so a, ɛsɛ sɛ mfonini no tenten yɛ bɛyɛ 2000 px. Sɛ wubue mfonini no wɔ browser a ɛyɛ bicubic resampling wɔ pixel grid no so a, ɛbɛtumi ama ɛnyɛ yie. Windows Photo Viewer yɛ browser a yɛahu sɛ ɛyɛ adwuma no yie.
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/wehAxodpQGg]
 
 
Sɛ nhwɛsoɔ foforɔ a ɛkyerɛ sɛnea wɔde saa nnyinasosɛm yi di dwuma no, mayɛ mfonini bi a ɛwɔ kɔla ne nkyerɛaseɛ a ɛne ne ho nhyia.

Nsɛm a wɔasesɛ
 
 Ɔsannaa 2018 
 
 
 
Nnipa dodoɔ bɛn na wɔfa wɔn so ansa na nsɛm a yɛhu wɔ intanɛt so adu yɛn nkyɛn? Ɛyɛ nokware anaa? Wiase a yɛhu wɔ yɛn foni, kɔmputa ne tɛlevihyɛn so no nsonsonoeɛ dodoɔ bɛn na ɛwɔ hɔ?
 
Ɛho hia sɛ yɛhwɛ nsɛm biara a yɛhu no fi nnyinasoɔ ahodoɔ pii so na yɛnnyae yɛn adwene nsi so sɛ ɛyɛ nokware.
 
Wɔyɛɛ adwuma yi denam mfonini baako a wɔkyeree no, na afei wɔsan kɔkyeree saa mfonini koro no ara bio wɔ kɔmputa so.

 Oforisuo 2018

TITLE: Nokware nnomma a nnipa paw
SLUG: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2018-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem.mdx
BUILT_HTML: nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem/index.html
============================================================
Nokware nnomma a nnipa paw
 
 Oforisuo 2018 
 
 
 
Wɔ saa adwuma yi mu no, wɔde nsɛmmisa krataa maa nnipa bi. Wɔ saa nsɛmmisa krataa yi mu no, nnipa a wɔrehwɛ wɔn no kyerɛw nnomma ɔha a ɛyɛ nokware a ɛwɔ 1-100 mu, a ɛbaa wɔn adwenem. Afei mehyɛɛ saa nneɛma yi kɔɔ kɔmputa mu na meyɛɛ girafu baako fi nsɛmmisa krataa biara mu. Horizonta x-axis no kyerɛ nnomma no nhyehyɛe, na vertikali y-axis no kyerɛ nnomma a wɔapaw no bo. Girafu a ɛyɛ bruu no yɛ kɔmputa na ɛyɛe, kyerɛ sɛ, nnomma a ɛyɛ nokware a ɛba no ara pɛ. Sɛ yɛhwɛ girafu nyinaa bom a, yebetumi ahu sɛ obiara a wɔrehwɛ no wɔ girafu a ɛyɛ soronko a ɛwɔ ne kwan a ɔfa so kyerɛw nnomma no. Yebetumi nso ahu sɛ wɔ girafu pii mu no, nkyekyɛmu bi a ɛyɛ pɛ koraa na ɛsan ba (mpii no, ɛyɛ nnomma a ɛkɔ soro ne nea ɛba fam no kwan).
 
NNOMMA A KƆMPUTA A ƐYƐ NOKWARE A ƐBA NO ARA PƐ PAW
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg | Girafu a ɛkyerɛ nnomma a ɛyɛ nokware a kɔmputa paw]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
NNOMMA A NNIPA PAW
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-2.jpg | Girafu a ɛkyerɛ nnomma a ɛyɛ nokware a nnipa paw, Hana Antolová (71 mfe)]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-3.jpg | Girafu a ɛkyerɛ nnomma a ɛyɛ nokware a nnipa paw, Lukáš Plescher (18 mfe)]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-3.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-4.jpg | Girafu a ɛkyerɛ nnomma a ɛyɛ nokware a nnipa paw, Natálie Bártková (16 mfe)]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-4.jpg] 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-5.jpg | Girafu a ɛkyerɛ nnomma a ɛyɛ nokware a nnipa paw, Michaela Jedličková (16 mfe)]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-5.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-6.jpg | Girafu a ɛkyerɛ nnomma a ɛyɛ nokware a nnipa paw, Alena Maurová (53 mfe)]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-6.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-7.jpg | Girafu a ɛkyerɛ nnomma a ɛyɛ nokware a nnipa paw, Daniel Šírek (16 mfe)]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/nahodna-cisla-zvolena-clovekem-obr-7.jpg]

Ahunmu a ɛnyɛ pɛ
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2018 
 
 
 
 
[VideoObject: https://www.youtube-nocookie.com/embed/khCWjid0QeU?rel=0&showinfo=0]
 
 
Wɔ dwom a ɛkyɛ simma anan yi mu no, ɛwɔ agyinamoa a ɔrewu nne. Wɔ nne a wɔakyerɛw no mu no, wotumi te nne titiriw mmienu: ahunmu a ɛnyɛ pɛ ne akoma a ɛrebɔ a ɛnyɛ den.

BUILT_HTML: z-pohledu-musle/index.html
============================================================
Sɛnea ɛteɛ wɔ mpaboa ani
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2018 
 
 
 
Video yi fa adwene si sɛnea mpaboa te nka ne nea ɛfa mu so. Ne adwene a ɛwɔ wiase ho nni hwee nni nea onipa wɔ so! Wɔ video a edi kan no mu no, wubetumi ahu ɛpo no ho mfonini a edi kan. Wɔ video a ɛtɔ so mmienu no mu no, wɔkyerɛ kɔla a ɛwɔ mfonini no mu wɔ afa benkum no so. Wɔ afa nifa no so no, ɛhɔ na RGB graph a ɛkyerɛ saa kɔla no wɔ.

Fom
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2017 
 
 
 
Sɛesɛɛ a ɛwɔ mfonini yi mu kyerɛ fom a ɛwɔ me German kasa nwoma no mu. Wɔahyehyɛ mfonini no sɛnea kratafa no di n'akyi pɛpɛɛpɛ. Eyi ma mfonini biara nya nhyehyɛe a ɛyɛ nwanwa na ɛyɛ nneɛma a wɔbɔ mfra.

TITLE: Rastɛr a ɛyɛ nhyehyɛe no a wɔasɛe no
SLUG: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-10-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru.mdx
BUILT_HTML: naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru/index.html
============================================================
Rastɛr a ɛyɛ nhyehyɛe no a wɔasɛe no
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2017 
 
 
 
Sɛisei yi mu no, mebɔɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛyɛ nneɛma a ɛyɛ ahwɛyiye ahorow a ɛnyɛ nea ɛda adi pɛpɛɛpɛ. Mepaeɛ nhoma no mu yɛɛ mmuada anan. Mmuada a edikan no mu no, meyɛɛ grid a ɛyɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ a ɛyɛ fapem, mmuada a ɛtɔ so mmienu no mu no, megyinaa grid no so kɔɔ anim na mehyɛɛ aseɛ sɛ meresa so kakra a ɛde ahoɔfannaa baeɛ. Mmuada a ɛtɔ so mmiɛnsa no mu no, mede nneɛma a ɛnyɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ na ɛyɛ nhyehyɛeɛ a ɛne ne ho hyia na ɛyɛ nwanwa bɛsɛee grid a ɛyɛ pɛpɛɛpɛ no. Na mmuada a ɛtɔ so anan a ɛyɛ nea etwa to no mu no, meyɛɛ nneɛma a ɛyɛ nwanwa a ɛwɔ mu no sɛnea ɛbɛyɛ a ɛbɛtumi ayɛ anim.
 
Fa fã bi:
 ADWENE A WƆDE GYINA SO
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg | Abstrakce 1.0 - nhyehyɛeɛ a ɛda hɔ]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-1.jpg] 




 
 
Fã mmienu:
 RASUTRE NOO MU AFIRI NKWAA NTESEE A EYI FI HƆ
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/naruseni-pravidelneho-rastru-obr-2.jpg | Abstrakce 2.0.0 - grid a ɛtumi akwantu a ɛbɛn nkyɛn bi a ɛteɛ a akwantu baako a ɛkeka akyerɛ no aka.]

============================================================
Nyanwuma
 
 Oforisuo 2017 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zmrtvychvstani-obr-1.jpg | mfonini a yɛansesa hwee]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zmrtvychvstani-obr-1.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/zmrtvychvstani-obr-2.jpg] 
 
 
Wɔ Kotonima 2016 mu no, mekyeree asiedie bi a ɛwɔ Morocco. ANSI koodu a ɛwɔ mfonini no mu no, mesesa nkyerɛkyerɛnsɛm a ɛyɛ nsew (†) nyinaa bɛyɛɛ nsoromma (*). Mfonini a mesesa no, mekyeree no bio.

TITLE: Ahwehwɛ mu kɔla a ɛyɛ pɛ
SLUG: barevny-prumer
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/barevny-prumer/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-04-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/barevny-prumer.mdx
BUILT_HTML: barevny-prumer/index.html
============================================================
Ahwehwɛ mu kɔla a ɛyɛ pɛ
 
 Oforisuo 2017 
 
 
 
Wɔ mfonini no benkum so no, wɔde 1156 mfonini a ɛwɔ 34x34 px raster mu na ɛhyɛɛ hɔ. Kɔla biara a ɛwɔ pixel no mu gyina hɔ ma mfonini biara a efi ɔman no mu kɔla a ɛyɛ pɛ. Wɔ mfonini no nifa so no, ɛyɛ kɔla a ɛyɛ pɛ a efi pixel nyinaa a ɛwɔ benkum so no mu, kyerɛ sɛ, mfonini nyinaa a efi ɔman no mu kɔla a ɛyɛ pɛ.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-1.png | Czech Republic]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-1.png] 
Czech Republic
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/barevny-prumer-obr-2.jpg | China]

Asukara
 
 Ɔpɛpɔn 2017 
 
 
 
Wɔyɛɛ mfonini ahorow yi bere a na wɔrehwɛ obiara a ɔpɛ sɛ ɔkɔ Střední odborné škole obchodu, užitého umění a designu a ɛwɔ Plzeň (Nerudova 33).
 
Mfonini a wɔde yɛɛ nhwehwɛmu no, na yebetumi de yɛn ankasa nneɛma aba, nanso na ɛwɔ sɛ ɛdɔɔso anaa ɛso yɛ hwee. Mide asukara adaka bi bae, a mede sii mpono so wɔ nhyehyɛe bi mu, na akyiri yi mede nsu guu so. Asukara no fii ase resɛe, ɛresɛe, na ɛregu kwan biara a ɛbɛba.

TITLE: Street art nhoma korabea
SLUG: street-art-knihovna
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/street-art-knihovna/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2017-03-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/street-art-knihovna.mdx
BUILT_HTML: street-art-knihovna/index.html
============================================================
Street art nhoma korabea
 
 Ɔpɛnimaa 2017 
 
 
 
Street art adwuma pii a ɛyɛ anigye wɔ yɛn ho mmeae nyinaa, efiri nkrataa nketenkete, afasu a wɔahyehyɛ ho nneɛma, kosi 3D nneɛma ahorow bi. Esiane sɛ ɛsɛ sɛ saa adwumayɛfo yi kura wɔn din kokoam esiane mmara a wɔbu so nti, street art wiase yi yɛ nea ɛnyɛ adwuma a wɔde hwehwɛ sika na ɛteɛ, na ɛne aguadi wiase no nni hwee yɛ. Street art a yebetumi ahu wɔ kuro no mu mmeae nyinaa no taa yɛ nea ɛyɛ foforo bere nyinaa. Street art foforo kata nea atwam so, nneɛma a wɔde ayɛ adwuma no ho nneɛma a ɛhwe, 3D nneɛma a wɔsɛe no, na nkrataa a wɔde kata ho no nso fi hɔ anaa ɛyɛ fitaa bere kɔ so. Ɛyɛ ɔkwan a ɛba no ankasa, bere a ɛwɔ so, nanso migye di sɛ street art bi fata sɛ wɔma ne nkwa tenten.
 
Enti na mepɛɛ sɛ mekyerɛw adwuma a ɛyɛ anigye sen biara no ho asɛm. Saa botae yi nti, mehyɛɛ Facebook kratafa bi ase wɔ afe 2017 mu a wɔfrɛ no Street art nhoma korabea, a meka nsɛm gu so kosi nnɛ. Sɛnea mebɛtumi adu nnipa pii nkyɛn no, mehyɛɛ Instagram kratafa a ɛwɔ din koro no ara ase.
 
Meyɛ mmoden sɛ mede adwuma a ɛwɔ adwene bi na ɛwɔ mu, na mibu adwuma no ho fɛ sɛ nea ɛba akyiri no, gu so wɔ saa nkratafa yi so.
 
Street art nhoma korabea (Facebook):
 www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.facebook.com/streetartknihovna]
 
Street art nhoma korabea (Instagram):
 www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna [https://www.instagram.com/streetartknihovna]
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg | Facebook kratafa no ho mfonini]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg | Instagram kratafa no ho mfonini]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/street-art-knihovna-obr-2.jpg]

============================================================
Ɛnyɛ hwee sɛ ɛhe na wofiri...
 
 11. 7. 2016 - 14. 8. 2016, Plzeň 
 
 DJKT (Nová scéna): Plzeňský salón 2016 
 
 
 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-1.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-2.jpg] 

  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/vystava-nezalezi-z-jakeho-kraje-jsi-prisel-2-obr-3.jpg] 
 
 
 
 
28. Ɔpɛpɔn 2014 na mekyerɛɛ mfonini bi a ɛwɔ ɔfasu a wɔakyerɛw so wɔ Mombasa. Afei mebɔɔ mmɔden sɛ mɛkyerɛ aseɛ nea ɛwɔ so no. Mepɛɛ ne fa bi a ɛda hɔ pefee wɔ intanɛt so na ɛnam saa so na mihuu mfonini a edidi so yi.
 

 

It doesn't matter which religion you claim you're. It doesn't matter which country you're coming from. It doesn't matter whether you 're poor or rich. It doesn't matter if you're black or white. We are all the same in the eyes of God.

 

 

Nezáleží, jakého jsi vyznání. Nezáleží, z jaké země pocházíš. Nezáleží, jestli jsi chudý nebo bohatý. Nezáleží, jestli jsi černoch nebo běloch. Všichni jsme si rovni v očích Boha.

 
 
 
 
Nkrataa a efi Plzeň TV:
 https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jxg-6iVPPmk

TITLE: Firi kuro no besi akwantu a ɛbaa
SLUG: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2016-01-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu.mdx
BUILT_HTML: od-mesta-az-po-nahodu/index.html
============================================================
Firi kuro no mu kosi akwantu a ɛbaa sae
 
 Ɔpɛnimaa 2016 
 
 
 
Wɔ afe 2016 mu, bere a merebo ne ho so ama ntoasoɔ sukuu a ɛfa nneyɛe ho (art school) wɔ ZUŠ Plzeň, Jagellonská 14, me wura no hyɛɛ me sɛ menya akwanya mfa nyɛ kuropɔn no mfoninitwa. Na mepɛ sɛ minim baabi a saa mfoninitwa yi betumi akodu, enti meyakoo saa adwuma yi kosii nnɛ (afe 2019). Efi mfoninitwa a ɛyɛ mmerɛw kosi sɛ mede me ho bɛhyɛɛ nnwuma a ɛyɛ nhyehyɛe a ɛsono ne su, a ɛde ntease a ɛyɛ nwanwa ba wɔ mfoninitwa no mu. Afei nso, mɛyɛɛ mmoden sɛ mede akwanya (chance) bɛdi dwuma wɔ saa adwuma yi mu, nanso mannya kwan ankasa a ɛnni ntease biara. Determinism (adeɛ biara a ɛfa kwan a ɛwɔ hɔ dedaw) na ɛda nkyɛn daa, enti mede pseudorandom (nkyerɛase a ɛte sɛ akwanya nanso ɛwɔ ntease) na edii dwuma. Akwan binom kyerɛɛ me kwan a ɛnni baabi a ɛkɔ, enti na ɛsɛ sɛ mehu akwan foforɔ ne mfoninitwa ho ntease foforɔ, te sɛ: ntease a ɛfa computer program, moiré effect, Fibonacci sequence, ne nea ɛkeka ho. Mfonini a edi soɔ yi yɛ saa nnwuma yi nyinaa nkabom.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-1.jpg | Mombasa: ɔkyerɛwfoɔ no mfonini ne ne nseɛm]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-1.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-2.jpg | Kurokyi neɛma a ɛwɔ kuro no mu: nkyekyɛmu a ɛwɔ nsaase no so]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-2.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-3.jpg | Kurokuro a kuro no nhyehyɛe: nsakraeɛ a ɛba nhyehyɛeɛ a ɛkɔ so daa mu]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-3.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-4.jpg | Kurotɔ no stil: mfirimu a ɛkyerɛ sɛ biribi ayɛ nteɛɛ mu nsakrae]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-4.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-5.jpg | Kuro kuro noo nneɛma a ɛteɛ: ɛkwan a wɔfa so dane adan ne akwan a ɛwɔ kuro no mu wɔ akwan soronko so]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-5.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-6.jpg | Kuro a nkuro a ɛwɔ abankɛseɛ mu: nsusuansoɔ a ɛba kwan a ɛnyɛ adwene ho wɔ nhyehyɛeɛ a ɛwɔ nhwɛsoɔ mu]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-6.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-7.jpg | Kuroku a kurow no nkyerɛkyerɛ mu: nkyerɛase a ɛyɛ mmerɛ ne nantew a ɛba no kwan so]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-7.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-8.jpg | Kuro a kuro no kwan no kwan: nneɛma a ɛba saa ara pɛ a wɔde kyerɛ kuro no]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-8.jpg] 
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-9.jpg | Kurokuro a nkurow no nhyehyɛe: RGB pixel a ɛba kwan kwan, moaré mfitiase]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-9.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-10.jpg]

  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/od-mesta-az-po-nahodu-obr-11.jpg]

TITLE: Nnua a ɛnyɛ
SLUG: plevel
URL: https://vojtamaur.cz/plevel/
LANGUAGE: ak
SOURCE_LANGUAGE: cs
SECTION: volna-tvorba
DATE: 2008-05-01
SOURCE: src/content/posts/plevel.mdx
BUILT_HTML: plevel/index.html
============================================================
Nnua a ɛnyɛ
 
 Kotonima 2008 
 
 
 
Adwuma a meyɛe wɔ sukuu a yɛkyerɛ mmofra adeɛ.
 
Wɔka kyerɛɛ yɛn sɛ yɛntwitwa nhwiren a ɛreyɛ afifi firi krataa a ɛwɔ ahosuo ahorow mu na yɛmfa nyɛ mfonini.
 
Mmom nhwiren no, me na meyɛɛ plevel.
 
  
[ImageObject: https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg | plevel]
  [https://vojtamaur.cz/images/plevel-obr-1.jpg]
